  takerRatio: number; // volumen taker compra/venta en futuros (1h)
  oiChange24h: number; // %
  oiSlope5m: number; // pendiente del OI en 2.5h (%)
  priceChange24h: number; // %
  premium: number; // (mark − index)/index → prima de futuros
  atr1h: number; // ATR en unidades de precio por hora
  liveLongLiq: number; // USDT liquidados a longs en la sesión
  liveShortLiq: number;
  cvdPct: number; // delta neto / volumen total de la ventana
  cvdDiv: "bear" | "bull" | null;
  oiUsdt: number; // OI nocional en USDT (0 = desconocido)
  fastSlopePct: number; // tendencia del tercio reciente (%)
  slowSlopePct: number; // tendencia de toda la ventana (%)
  momPct?: number; // impulso de las últimas velas (%) → evita llamar giros prematuros
  // --- refuerzos añadidos ---
  bookImbalance: number; // ratio bid/ask del libro (≈1 = equilibrado, >1 = muro comprador)
  xCfundingGap: number; // funding Binance − media(OKX+Bybit) → sesgo de apalancamiento del venue
  fundingWindow: number; // 0..1 proximidad al settlement de funding (cada 8h UTC)
  sweep: number; // −1..1 detección de barrida reciente (mecha a través de un cluster)
  liqVelocity: number; // −1..1 aceleración de liquidaciones en los últimos minutos
  optionsPutCall: number;
  optionsAtmIv?: number;
  optionsSkew25d?: number;
  optionsMaxPain?: number;
  optionsOiChangePct?: number | null;
  spotCvdPct?: number;
  futuresCvdPct?: number;
  futuresCvdDiv?: "bear" | "bull" | null;
  bookDelta?: number;
  bookPersistence?: number;
  absorptionScore?: number;
  realSweep?: number;
  liqBurst?: number;
  liqAcceleration?: number;
  crossOiChangePct?: number;
  sourcesCriticalOk?: boolean;
  weights?: Record<string, number>;
  liquidityAdded?: number;
  liquidityRemoved?: number;
  addRemoveImbalance?: number;
  spoofRisk?: number;
  poc?: number;
  vwap?: number;
  liqRecentNotional?: number;
  liqBaselineNotional?: number;
  liqRatio?: number;
  sourceQuality?: number;
  liqFlowQuality?: "live" | "degraded";
  externalClusterScore?: number;
}

const clamp = (v: number, lo = -1, hi = 1) => Math.min(hi, Math.max(lo, v));

/* ------------------------------------------------------------
   Distancia real de liquidación con margen de mantenimiento
   Binance BTC tier 1: MMR 0.40%. Distancia = 1/L − MMR.
   10x → 9.6% · 25x → 3.6% · 50x → 1.6% · 100x → 0.6%
   ------------------------------------------------------------ */
export const liqDistance = (L: number) => Math.max(1 / Math.max(L,1) - 0.004, 0.001);

/* ------------------------------------------------------------
   CVD — volumen delta acumulado (compra agresiva vs venta)
   ------------------------------------------------------------ */
export interface CvdInfo {
  series: number[];
  cvdPct: number; // -1..1
  cvdNet: number; // USDT netos
  divergence: "bear" | "bull" | null;
}

export function computeCvd(candles: Candle[]): CvdInfo {
  let cvd = 0;
  let totalQ = 0;
  const series: number[] = [];
  for (const c of candles) {
    const buy = c.takerBuyQuote ?? c.quoteVolume * 0.5;
    const delta = 2 * buy - c.quoteVolume;
    cvd += delta;
    totalQ += c.quoteVolume;
    series.push(cvd);
  }
  const cvdPct = totalQ > 0 ? cvd / totalQ : 0;
  const n = candles.length;
  let divergence: "bear" | "bull" | null = null;
  if (n >= 9) {
    const third = Math.floor(n / 3);
    const priceNow = candles[n - 1].close;
    const priceMid = candles[n - 1 - third].close;
    const cvdNow = series[n - 1];
    const cvdMid = series[n - 1 - third];
    if (priceNow > priceMid * 1.002 && cvdNow < cvdMid) divergence = "bear";
    else if (priceNow < priceMid * 0.998 && cvdNow > cvdMid) divergence = "bull";
  }
  return { series, cvdPct, cvdNet: cvd, divergence };
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------
   Mapa de liquidación estimado (velas + apalancamiento + OI)
   ------------------------------------------------------------ */
export function estimateLiquidationMap(
  candles: Candle[],
  spot: number,
  leverages: number[],
  rangePct: number,
  binsCount = 58,
  oiUsdt = 0,
  nearPct = 2
): { bins: LiqBin[]; longPool: number; shortPool: number; nearLongPool: number; nearShortPool: number; clusters: Cluster[] } {
  const model = estimateLiquidationClusters({
    candles,
    spot,
    leverages,
    rangePct,
    binsCount,
    oiContracts: spot > 0 ? oiUsdt / spot : 0,
    priceForOi: spot,
    nearPct,
  });
  return {
    bins: model.bins,
    longPool: model.longPool,
    shortPool: model.shortPool,
    nearLongPool: model.nearLongPool,
    nearShortPool: model.nearShortPool,
    clusters: model.clusters,
  };
}

export function atrOf(candles: Candle[]): number {
  if (candles.length < 2) return 0;
  let sum = 0;
  const k = Math.min(24, candles.length - 1);
  for (let i = candles.length - k; i < candles.length; i++) {
    const c = candles[i];
    const prev = candles[i - 1];
    sum += Math.max(c.high - c.low, Math.abs(c.high - prev.close), Math.abs(c.low - prev.close));
  }
  return sum / k;
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------
   Régimen de volatilidad (ATR como % del precio por hora).
   Clasifica el mercado para ajustar ventana temporal y confianza.
   ------------------------------------------------------------ */
export type Regime = "calm" | "normal" | "high" | "extreme";

export interface RegimeInfo {
  regime: Regime;
  atrPct: number; // ATR/h como % del spot
  label: string;
  color: string;
  windowScale: number; // multiplicador de la ventana temporal (alta vol = señales más rápidas)
  confAdj: number; // ajuste adicional de confianza
  note: string;
}

export type MarketRegime =
  | "trend_up"
  | "trend_down"
  | "long_build"
  | "short_build"
  | "short_squeeze"
  | "long_squeeze"
  | "accumulation"
  | "distribution"
  | "compression"
  | "expansion"
  | "neutral";

export interface MarketRegimeInfo {
  regime: MarketRegime;
  label: string;
  direction: "up" | "down" | "neutral";
  strength: number;
  evidence: string[];
  note: string;
}

/**
 * Determina primero el estado del mercado usando evidencia independiente.
 * No emite LONG/SHORT por sí mismo; describe el escenario que luego usa el veredicto.
 */
export function classifyMarketRegime(inp: Pick<VerdictInput,
  "fastSlopePct" | "slowSlopePct" | "momPct" | "oiSlope5m" | "priceChange24h" |
  "fundingRate" | "takerRatio" | "spotCvdPct" | "futuresCvdPct" | "absorptionScore" |
  "liqBurst" | "liqAcceleration" | "liveLongLiq" | "liveShortLiq" | "bookImbalance" |
  "realSweep" | "addRemoveImbalance">): MarketRegimeInfo {
  const px = inp.momPct ?? inp.priceChange24h;
  const trend = 0.55 * Math.tanh(inp.fastSlopePct / 0.8) + 0.45 * Math.tanh(inp.slowSlopePct / 2);
  const cvd = 0.5 * Math.tanh((inp.spotCvdPct ?? 0) * 2.8) + 0.5 * Math.tanh((inp.futuresCvdPct ?? 0) * 2.8);
  const taker = Math.tanh((inp.takerRatio - 1) * 1.8);
  const oi = Math.tanh((inp.oiSlope5m ?? 0) / 0.6);
  const liqTotal = Math.max(1, inp.liveLongLiq + inp.liveShortLiq);
  const liqImb = (inp.liveShortLiq - inp.liveLongLiq) / liqTotal;
  const liqPressure = Math.tanh(liqImb * 2.2);
  const abs = inp.absorptionScore ?? 0;
  const book = Math.tanh(((inp.bookImbalance ?? 1) - 1) * 2);
  const extremes = Math.min(1, Math.abs(inp.fundingRate) / 0.0015);
  const evidence: string[] = [];

  // Squeeze: liquidación fuerte + OI cayendo/reestructurándose + dirección del flujo.
  if ((inp.liqBurst ?? 0) > 0.7 && inp.liveShortLiq > inp.liveLongLiq * 1.35 && px > 0) {
    evidence.push("shorts siendo liquidados");
    if ((inp.liqAcceleration ?? 0) > 0.4) evidence.push("aceleración de liquidaciones");
    if (cvd > 0.2) evidence.push("CVD positivo");
    return { regime: "short_squeeze", label: "SHORT SQUEEZE", direction: "up", strength: clamp(0.65 + 0.2 * Math.abs(cvd) + 0.15 * Math.min(1, inp.liqBurst ?? 0)), evidence, note: "Compresión de shorts con liquidaciones alcistas observadas; el mecanismo favorece continuación si el flujo persiste." };
  }
  if ((inp.liqBurst ?? 0) > 0.7 && inp.liveLongLiq > inp.liveShortLiq * 1.35 && px < 0) {
    evidence.push("longs siendo liquidados");
    if ((inp.liqAcceleration ?? 0) > 0.4) evidence.push("aceleración de liquidaciones");
    if (cvd < -0.2) evidence.push("CVD negativo");
    return { regime: "long_squeeze", label: "LONG SQUEEZE", direction: "down", strength: clamp(0.65 + 0.2 * Math.abs(cvd) + 0.15 * Math.min(1, inp.liqBurst ?? 0)), evidence, note: "Desapalancamiento de longs con liquidaciones bajistas observadas; el mecanismo favorece continuación si el flujo persiste." };
  }

  if (Math.abs(trend) < 0.12 && Math.abs(oi) < 0.2 && Math.abs(cvd) < 0.2 && Math.abs(taker) < 0.18) {
    evidence.push("precio y flujo comprimidos");
    return { regime: "compression", label: "COMPRESIÓN", direction: "neutral", strength: clamp(1 - (Math.abs(trend)+Math.abs(oi)+Math.abs(cvd))/3), evidence, note: "Baja expansión de precio, OI y flujo; espera ruptura confirmada en lugar de anticiparla." };
  }

  if (oi > 0.35 && trend > 0.2 && cvd > 0.15 && taker > 0.15) {
    evidence.push("precio + OI + CVD suben");
    if (inp.absorptionScore && inp.absorptionScore > 0.35) evidence.push("absorción compradora");
    return { regime: "long_build", label: "LONG BUILD", direction: "up", strength: clamp(0.45 + 0.2*Math.abs(trend) + 0.2*Math.abs(oi) + 0.15*Math.abs(cvd)), evidence, note: "Construcción de interés abierto acompañada por flujo alcista; estructura favorable mientras no aparezca distribución." };
  }
  if (oi > 0.35 && trend < -0.2 && cvd < -0.15 && taker < -0.15) {
    evidence.push("precio ↓ + OI ↑ + CVD ↓");
    if (inp.absorptionScore && inp.absorptionScore < -0.35) evidence.push("absorción vendedora");
    return { regime: "short_build", label: "SHORT BUILD", direction: "down", strength: clamp(0.45 + 0.2*Math.abs(trend) + 0.2*Math.abs(oi) + 0.15*Math.abs(cvd)), evidence, note: "Construcción de interés abierto acompañada por flujo bajista; estructura favorable a shorts mientras no aparezca absorción alcista." };
  }

  if (trend > 0.28 && cvd > 0.2) {
    evidence.push("estructura alcista", "CVD positivo");
    if (book > 0.15) evidence.push("bid depth favorable");
    return { regime: "trend_up", label: "TENDENCIA ALCISTA", direction: "up", strength: clamp(0.5 + Math.abs(trend)*0.3 + Math.abs(cvd)*0.2), evidence, note: "La estructura y el flujo apuntan arriba; prioriza compras en retrocesos y evita contrarian sin evidencia." };
  }
  if (trend < -0.28 && cvd < -0.2) {
    evidence.push("estructura bajista", "CVD negativo");
    if (book < -0.15) evidence.push("ask depth favorable");
    return { regime: "trend_down", label: "TENDENCIA BAJISTA", direction: "down", strength: clamp(0.5 + Math.abs(trend)*0.3 + Math.abs(cvd)*0.2), evidence, note: "La estructura y el flujo apuntan abajo; prioriza ventas en rebotes y evita contrarian sin evidencia." };
  }

  if (abs > 0.35 && trend < 0 && cvd > 0) {
    evidence.push("ventas agresivas absorbidas", "CVD positivo");
    return { regime: "accumulation", label: "ACUMULACIÓN", direction: "up", strength: clamp(0.45 + Math.abs(abs)*0.35 + cvd*0.2), evidence, note: "La presión vendedora no consigue desplazar el precio y aparece absorción compradora." };
  }
  if (abs < -0.35 && trend > 0 && cvd < 0) {
    evidence.push("compras agresivas absorbidas", "CVD negativo");
    return { regime: "distribution", label: "DISTRIBUCIÓN", direction: "down", strength: clamp(0.45 + Math.abs(abs)*0.35 + Math.abs(cvd)*0.2), evidence, note: "La presión compradora no consigue extender el precio y aparece absorción vendedora." };
  }

  const expansion = (inp.liqBurst ?? 0) > 0.45 || Math.abs(oi) > 0.45 || Math.abs(taker) > 0.45 || extremes > 0.7;
  if (expansion) {
    evidence.push("flujo/volatilidad expandiéndose");
    const direction = (trend + cvd + taker + book) > 0.25 ? "up" : (trend + cvd + taker + book) < -0.25 ? "down" : "neutral";
    return { regime: "expansion", label: "EXPANSIÓN", direction, strength: clamp(0.45 + Math.abs(trend+cvd+taker)*0.12), evidence, note: "El mercado está expandiendo flujo; espera confirmación de dirección antes de aumentar exposición." };
  }

  evidence.push("sin dominancia clara");
  return { regime: "neutral", label: "NEUTRAL", direction: "neutral", strength: 0.25, evidence, note: "Las fuentes independientes no presentan una historia dominante; no fuerces una señal." };
}

export function classifyRegime(atrPct: number): RegimeInfo {
  if (atrPct < 0.15) {
    return {
      regime: "calm",
      atrPct,
      label: "CALMA",
      color: "#3fb6ff",
      windowScale: 1.5,
      confAdj: 0,
      note: "Baja volatilidad: los sweeps tardan más en materializarse; amplía la ventana y ten paciencia.",
    };
  }
  if (atrPct < 0.45) {
    return {
      regime: "normal",
      atrPct,
      label: "NORMAL",
      color: "#2fd6a5",
      windowScale: 1,
      confAdj: 0,
      note: "Volatilidad estándar: la ventana temporal del radar aplica tal cual.",
    };
  }
  if (atrPct < 0.9) {
    return {
      regime: "high",
      atrPct,
      label: "ALTA",
      color: "#ffb547",
      windowScale: 0.6,
      confAdj: -6,
      note: "Volatilidad alta: las barridas son rápidas y violentas; ventana acortada y confianza penalizada.",
    };
  }
  return {
    regime: "extreme",
    atrPct,
    label: "EXTREMA",
    color: "#ff4d6d",
    windowScale: 0.4,
    confAdj: -12,
    note: "Volatilidad extrema: el ruido domina; reduce tamaño, amplía stops y desconfía de señales aisladas.",
  };
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------
   Auxiliares de los factores de refuerzo
   ------------------------------------------------------------ */

/* Proximidad al settlement de funding (0, 8 y 16 UTC). Devuelve 0..1
   donde 1 = justo en la ventana (±1h). */
export function fundingProximity(nowMs: number): number {
  const h = (nowMs / 3600_000) % 8; // horas desde el último settlement
  const dist = Math.min(h, 8 - h); // distancia al settlement más cercano
  return Math.max(0, 1 - dist / 1.25);
}

/* Detección de barrida: en las últimas velas, ¿la mecha atravesó el cluster
   más cercano y el cierre volvió? >0 = barrieron shorts (alcista gastado),
   <0 = barrieron longs (bajista gastado). */
export function detectSweep(candles: Candle[], clusters: Cluster[], lookback = 6): number {
  if (candles.length < lookback + 1 || clusters.length === 0) return 0;
  const spot = candles[candles.length - 1].close;
  // cluster más cercano al spot
  const near = clusters.reduce((best, c) =>
    Math.abs(c.price - spot) < Math.abs(best.price - spot) ? c : best
  );
  let out = 0;
  for (let i = Math.max(0, candles.length - lookback); i < candles.length; i++) {
    const c = candles[i];
    if (near.price > spot) {
      // cluster arriba (shorts): mecha que lo cruza y cierra abajo = barrida de shorts
      if (c.high >= near.price && c.close < near.price) out = Math.max(out, 0.8);
    } else {
      // cluster abajo (longs): mecha que lo cruza y cierra arriba = barrida de longs
      if (c.low <= near.price && c.close > near.price) out = Math.min(out === 0 ? -0.8 : out, -0.8);
    }
  }
  return out;
}

/* Velocidad de liquidación: compara el desequilibrio long/short de los últimos
   minutos contra el de toda la sesión. >0 = se están quemando longs ahora. */
export function liqVelocityScore(events: LiqEvent[], nowMs: number, recentMs = 5 * 60_000): number {
  if (events.length < 3) return 0;
  let rL = 0, rS = 0, tL = 0, tS = 0;
  for (const e of events) {
    const isLong = e.side === "long";
    if (isLong) tL += e.notional; else tS += e.notional;
    if (nowMs - e.time <= recentMs) {
      if (isLong) rL += e.notional; else rS += e.notional;
    }
  }
  const rt = rL + rS;
  const tt = tL + tS;
  if (rt < 100_000 || tt === 0) return 0; // sin cascada reciente significativa
  const recentImb = (rL - rS) / rt;
  const totalImb = (tL - tS) / tt;
  return clamp((recentImb - totalImb) * 1.6 + recentImb * 0.5);
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------
   Motor de veredicto: dirección esperada del precio. El squeeze es el mecanismo, no la dirección.
   ------------------------------------------------------------ */
export function computeVerdict(inp: VerdictInput): Verdict {
  // Sanitización defensiva: ninguna métrica inválida puede contaminar todo el veredicto con NaN.
  const finite = (v: unknown, fallback = 0): number => {
    const n = Number(v);
    return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : fallback;
  };
  inp = {
    ...inp,
    spot: finite(inp.spot),
    longPool: finite(inp.longPool),
    shortPool: finite(inp.shortPool),
    nearLongPool: finite(inp.nearLongPool),
    nearShortPool: finite(inp.nearShortPool),
    fundingRate: finite(inp.fundingRate),
    fundingTrend: finite(inp.fundingTrend),
    globalRatio: finite(inp.globalRatio, 1),
    topRatio: finite(inp.topRatio, 1),
    topRatioDeltaPct: inp.topRatioDeltaPct == null ? inp.topRatioDeltaPct : finite(inp.topRatioDeltaPct),
    topPositionConfidence: finite(inp.topPositionConfidence, 0),
    crossLiqLong: finite(inp.crossLiqLong, 0),
    crossLiqShort: finite(inp.crossLiqShort, 0),
    crossLiqTotal: finite(inp.crossLiqTotal, 0),
    takerRatio: finite(inp.takerRatio, 1),
    oiChange24h: finite(inp.oiChange24h),
    oiSlope5m: finite(inp.oiSlope5m),
    priceChange24h: finite(inp.priceChange24h),
    premium: finite(inp.premium),
    atr1h: finite(inp.atr1h),
    liveLongLiq: finite(inp.liveLongLiq),
    liveShortLiq: finite(inp.liveShortLiq),
    cvdPct: finite(inp.cvdPct),
    spotCvdPct: finite(inp.spotCvdPct),
    futuresCvdPct: finite(inp.futuresCvdPct),
    bookImbalance: finite(inp.bookImbalance, 1),
    bookDelta: finite(inp.bookDelta),
    bookPersistence: finite(inp.bookPersistence),
    absorptionScore: finite(inp.absorptionScore),
    realSweep: finite(inp.realSweep),
    liqBurst: finite(inp.liqBurst),
    liqAcceleration: finite(inp.liqAcceleration),
    xCfundingGap: finite(inp.xCfundingGap),
    crossOiChangePct: finite(inp.crossOiChangePct),
    liquidityAdded: finite(inp.liquidityAdded),
    liquidityRemoved: finite(inp.liquidityRemoved),
    addRemoveImbalance: finite(inp.addRemoveImbalance),
    spoofRisk: finite(inp.spoofRisk),
    poc: finite(inp.poc),
    vwap: finite(inp.vwap),
    liqRecentNotional: finite(inp.liqRecentNotional),
    liqBaselineNotional: finite(inp.liqBaselineNotional),
    liqRatio: finite(inp.liqRatio),
    externalClusterScore: finite(inp.externalClusterScore),
    optionsPutCall: finite(inp.optionsPutCall, 1),
    optionsAtmIv: finite(inp.optionsAtmIv),
    optionsSkew25d: finite(inp.optionsSkew25d),
    optionsMaxPain: finite(inp.optionsMaxPain),
    optionsOiChangePct: inp.optionsOiChangePct == null ? inp.optionsOiChangePct : finite(inp.optionsOiChangePct),
    fastSlopePct: finite(inp.fastSlopePct),
    slowSlopePct: finite(inp.slowSlopePct),
    momPct: inp.momPct == null ? inp.momPct : finite(inp.momPct),
  };
  const factors:Factor[]=[]; const warnings:Warning[]=[];
  const atrPct=inp.spot>0?inp.atr1h/inp.spot*100:0;
  const regime=classifyRegime(atrPct);
  const marketRegime=classifyMarketRegime(inp);
  const lp=liquidityPull(inp.clusters,atrPct);
  const add=(id:string,label:string,detail:string,score:number,weight:number,school:'contrarian'|'momentum')=>{
    factors.push({id,label,detail,score:clamp(score),weight,school});
  };
  const px=inp.momPct??inp.priceChange24h;

  const structure=clamp(0.6*Math.tanh(inp.fastSlopePct/0.8)+0.4*Math.tanh(inp.slowSlopePct/2));
  add('structure','Estructura',`${inp.fastSlopePct>=0?'↑':'↓'}${Math.abs(inp.fastSlopePct)?.toFixed?.(2)}% corto · ${inp.slowSlopePct>=0?'↑':'↓'}${Math.abs(inp.slowSlopePct)?.toFixed?.(2)}% fondo`,structure,0.07,'momentum');

  let oiScore=0,oiRegime='NEUTRAL';
  if(inp.oiSlope5m>0.25&&px>0.15){oiScore=0.8;oiRegime='LONG BUILD';}
  else if(inp.oiSlope5m>0.25&&px<-0.15){oiScore=-0.8;oiRegime='SHORT BUILD';}
  else if(inp.oiSlope5m<-0.25&&px>0.15){oiScore=0.45;oiRegime='SHORT UNWIND';}
  else if(inp.oiSlope5m<-0.25&&px<-0.15){oiScore=-0.45;oiRegime='LONG UNWIND';}
  add('oiRegime','Régimen OI',`${oiRegime} · OI 24h ${inp.oiChange24h>=0?'+':''}${inp.oiChange24h?.toFixed?.(1)}% · 5m slope ${inp.oiSlope5m>=0?'+':''}${inp.oiSlope5m?.toFixed?.(2)}%`,oiScore,0.12,'momentum');

  const fundingScore=clamp(-Math.tanh(inp.fundingRate/0.00045));
  add('funding','Funding',`${(inp.fundingRate*100)?.toFixed?.(4)}% · ${inp.fundingRate>=0?'longs pagan':'shorts pagan'}`,fundingScore,0.08,'contrarian');
  if(Math.abs(inp.fundingRate)>=0.001)warnings.push({tone:'warn',text:'Funding extremo: el posicionamiento contrarian gana peso, pero no determina dirección por sí solo.'});

  const posScore=clamp(0.7*Math.tanh((1/(Math.max(inp.topRatio,0.01))-1)*1.8)+0.3*Math.tanh((1/(Math.max(inp.globalRatio,0.01))-1)*1.1));
  const posDelta = inp.topRatioDeltaPct ?? null;
  const posFlowScore = posDelta == null ? 0 : clamp(Math.tanh(posDelta/4));
  const posCombined = clamp(posScore*0.55 + posFlowScore*0.45);
  add('positioning','Top Trader Position Flow',`Top ${inp.topRatio?.toFixed?.(2)}× · Δ ${posDelta == null ? '—' : (posDelta>=0?'+':'')+posDelta?.toFixed?.(2)+'%'} · extremidad estadística ${((inp.topPositionConfidence??0)*100)?.toFixed?.(0)}%`,posCombined,0.10,'contrarian');

  add('taker','Taker flow',`Ratio compra/venta ${inp.takerRatio?.toFixed?.(2)} · tendencia ${inp.takerTrend>=0?'+':''}${inp.takerTrend?.toFixed?.(2)}`,clamp(Math.tanh((inp.takerRatio-1)*2.2)+0.25*Math.tanh(inp.takerTrend*2)),0.10,'momentum');

  const spotCvd=inp.spotCvdPct??0, futCvd=inp.futuresCvdPct??inp.cvdPct;
  add('spotCvd','CVD spot',`Delta real ${(spotCvd*100)?.toFixed?.(2)}%`,clamp(spotCvd*3),0.08,'momentum');
  add('futuresCvd','CVD futuros',`Delta real ${(futCvd*100)?.toFixed?.(2)}%${inp.futuresCvdDiv?` · divergencia ${inp.futuresCvdDiv}`:''}`,clamp(futCvd*3),0.08,'momentum');

  const book=clamp(((inp.bookImbalance??1)-1)*1.35*0.55+(inp.bookDelta??0)*0.2+(inp.bookPersistence??0)*0.15+(inp.addRemoveImbalance??0)*0.1);
  add('orderFlow','Order flow L2',`Imbalance ${(inp.bookImbalance??1)?.toFixed?.(2)}× · delta ${(inp.bookDelta??0)?.toFixed?.(2)} · persistencia ${(inp.bookPersistence??0)?.toFixed?.(2)}`,book,0.07,'momentum');

  const abs=inp.absorptionScore??0;
  add('absorption','Absorción',`${abs>0?'compras absorbidas':'ventas absorbidas'} · score ${abs?.toFixed?.(2)}`,abs,0.08,'contrarian');

  const ar=inp.addRemoveImbalance??0;
  add('liquidityFlow','Liquidez añadida/retirada',`add ${(inp.liquidityAdded??0)/1e6>0?(inp.liquidityAdded!/1e6)?.toFixed?.(1):'0'}M · pull ${(inp.liquidityRemoved??0)/1e6>0?(inp.liquidityRemoved!/1e6)?.toFixed?.(1):'0'}M · balance ${ar?.toFixed?.(2)}`,ar,0.06,'momentum');
  if((inp.spoofRisk??0)>0.55)warnings.push({tone:'warn',text:'Retirada rápida y desproporcionada de liquidez detectada. Es riesgo de spoof/pull, no una prueba de manipulación.'});

  const totalL=inp.liveLongLiq+inp.liveShortLiq;
  const liqImb=totalL>0?(inp.liveShortLiq-inp.liveLongLiq)/totalL:0;
  const liqScore=clamp(liqImb*0.75+(inp.liqAcceleration??0)*0.35);
  const observedCrossTotal=inp.crossLiqTotal??0; const crossLiqImb=observedCrossTotal>0?((inp.crossLiqShort??0)-(inp.crossLiqLong??0))/observedCrossTotal:0;
  const crossLiqScore=clamp(crossLiqImb*0.35);
  add('liquidationFlow','Liquidaciones observadas',`${(inp.liveShortLiq/1e6)?.toFixed?.(2)}M shorts · ${(inp.liveLongLiq/1e6)?.toFixed?.(2)}M longs · cross ${(observedCrossTotal/1e6)?.toFixed?.(1)}M · x${(inp.liqRatio??0)?.toFixed?.(1)} intensidad`,clamp(liqScore+crossLiqScore),0.13,'momentum');
  if((inp.liqBurst??0)>0.7)warnings.push({tone:'danger',text:'Cascada de liquidaciones observadas: el flujo tiene intensidad anormal.'});

  const clusterScore=clamp(lp.pull*0.8 + (inp.liqRatio&&inp.liqRatio>2?Math.sign(lp.pull)*0.12:0));
  const clusterConvergenceScore=inp.externalClusterScore??0;
  add('clusterConvergence','Convergencia externa',`${clusterConvergenceScore>0?'Hay coincidencias':'Sin fuente externa activa'} · score ${clusterConvergenceScore?.toFixed?.(2)}`,clusterConvergenceScore,0.06,'contrarian');
  add('clusters','Clusters estimados',lp.pull>0?'Mayor densidad estimada arriba, lado short':'Mayor densidad estimada abajo, lado long',clusterScore,0.09,'contrarian');

  const cross=clamp(-(inp.xCfundingGap??0)/0.0004*0.6 + (inp.crossOiChangePct??0)/10*0.4);
  add('crossVenue','Cross-exchange',`Funding spread ${(inp.xCfundingGap??0)*100}% · dispersión OI ${(inp.crossOiChangePct??0)?.toFixed?.(2)}%`,cross,0.06,'contrarian');

  add('volumeProfile','POC/VWAP',`POC ${inp.poc?fmtUsd(inp.poc):'n/d'} · VWAP ${inp.vwap?fmtUsd(inp.vwap):'n/d'}`,inp.poc&&inp.vwap?clamp((inp.spot-inp.poc)/Math.max(inp.atr1h,1)):0,0.04,'momentum');

  const optScore=clamp(
    (-(inp.optionsPutCall-1)/0.9)*0.55
    + clamp(-(inp.optionsSkew25d??0)/0.08)*0.30
    + (inp.optionsMaxPain&&inp.atr1h?clamp((inp.optionsMaxPain-inp.spot)/Math.max(inp.atr1h,1))*0.15:0)
  );
  add('options','Opciones',`P/C ${inp.optionsPutCall?.toFixed?.(2)} · IV ATM ${((inp.optionsAtmIv??0)*100)?.toFixed?.(1)}% · skew ${(inp.optionsSkew25d??0)?.toFixed?.(3)} · ΔOI ${inp.optionsOiChangePct == null ? "—" : inp.optionsOiChangePct?.toFixed?.(1)+"%"}`,optScore,0.03,'contrarian');

  if(inp.weights) for(const f of factors){const w=inp.weights[f.id];if(typeof w==='number'&&w>0)f.weight=w;}

  const c=factors.filter(f=>f.school==='contrarian'),m=factors.filter(f=>f.school==='momentum');
  const wc=c.reduce((a,f)=>a+f.weight,0)||1,wm=m.reduce((a,f)=>a+f.weight,0)||1;
  const contrarian=c.reduce((a,f)=>a+f.score*f.weight,0)/wc;
  const momentum=m.reduce((a,f)=>a+f.score*f.weight,0)/wm;

  const quality=[
    inp.sourcesCriticalOk!==false,
    (inp.futuresCvdPct!==undefined)&&(inp.spotCvdPct!==undefined),
    (inp.bookImbalance!==undefined),
    (inp.liqFlowQuality??'degraded')==='live'
  ].filter(Boolean).length/4;
  const gate=clamp(0.38 + Math.min(0.16,Math.abs(lp.pull)*0.12) + Math.min(0.10,Math.abs(inp.fundingRate)/0.001*0.1),0.32,0.62);
  const score=gate*contrarian+(1-gate)*momentum;
  const regimeAlignment = marketRegime.direction === "neutral" ? 0 : marketRegime.direction === "up" ? 0.18 : -0.18;
  const drive=0.50*score+0.32*lp.pull+0.18*regimeAlignment;
  let direction:Verdict['direction']=drive>0.12?'up':drive<-0.12?'down':'neutral';
  if (marketRegime.direction === "neutral" && (marketRegime.regime === "compression" || marketRegime.regime === "neutral" || marketRegime.strength < 0.55)) {
    direction = "neutral";
  } else if (marketRegime.direction !== "neutral" && marketRegime.strength >= 0.55) {
    const raw = marketRegime.direction === "up" ? 1 : -1;
    if (marketRegime.strength >= 0.72 && Math.sign(drive) !== raw) direction = "neutral";
    else if (marketRegime.strength >= 0.85) direction = raw > 0 ? "up" : "down";
  }

  const independentAgreement = factors.filter(f=>Math.abs(f.score)>=0.2&&direction!=='neutral'&&Math.sign(f.score)===Math.sign(drive)).length;
  if(direction!=='neutral' && independentAgreement<4) direction='neutral';

  if (marketRegime.regime === "short_squeeze" || marketRegime.regime === "long_squeeze") warnings.push({tone:"warn",text:`Régimen detectado: ${marketRegime.label}. El squeeze es el mecanismo; la dirección es ${marketRegime.direction === "up" ? "alcista" : "bajista"}.`});
  if ((marketRegime.regime === "compression" || marketRegime.regime === "neutral") && Math.abs(drive) < 0.2) warnings.push({tone:"warn",text:"Régimen sin expansión direccional clara: se evita forzar LONG/SHORT."});
  const harmony=Math.round(100-Math.min(100,Math.abs(contrarian-momentum)*70));
  let confidence=Math.round(Math.max(0,Math.min(96,35+Math.abs(drive)*52+independentAgreement*3+harmony*0.12+quality*7+regime.confAdj)));
  if(quality<0.75) confidence=Math.min(confidence,65);
  if(!inp.clusters.length) confidence=Math.min(confidence,45);
  if(inp.sourcesCriticalOk===false){direction='neutral';confidence=0;warnings.push({tone:'danger',text:'Datos críticos incompletos: señal bloqueada.'});}

  const shorts=inp.clusters.filter(c=>c.side==='short').sort((a,b)=>a.distancePct-b.distancePct);
  const longs=inp.clusters.filter(c=>c.side==='long').sort((a,b)=>a.distancePct-b.distancePct);
  const target=direction==='up'?(lp.up.cluster??shorts[0]??null):direction==='down'?(lp.down.cluster??longs[0]??null):null;
  const invalidation=direction==='up'?(lp.down.cluster??longs[0]??null):direction==='down'?(lp.up.cluster??shorts[0]??null):null;
  const cascade=atrPct?inp.clusters.filter(c=>c.distancePct<=atrPct).sort((a,b)=>a.distancePct-b.distancePct):[];
  if(cascade.length>=2)warnings.push({tone:'warn',text:`${cascade.length} clusters estimados dentro de 1 ATR.`});
  if((inp.realSweep??0)!==0)warnings.push({tone:'warn',text:inp.realSweep!>0?'Sweep estructural alcista: barrida abajo y recuperación.':'Sweep estructural bajista: barrida arriba y rechazo.'});
  if((inp.futuresCvdDiv==='bear'&&direction==='up')||(inp.futuresCvdDiv==='bull'&&direction==='down'))warnings.push({tone:'warn',text:'Divergencia del CVD de futuros contradice la dirección propuesta.'});

  const distance=target&&inp.atr1h?Math.abs(target.price-inp.spot)/inp.atr1h:4,ws=regime.windowScale;
  const windowH:[number,number]=[Math.max(1,Math.round(distance*.5*ws)),Math.min(96,Math.max(2,Math.round(distance*1.7*ws)))];
  const headline=direction==='up'?'LONG · MOVIMIENTO ALCISTA':direction==='down'?'SHORT · MOVIMIENTO BAJISTA':'NEUTRO · SIN SEÑAL';
  const sub=direction==='up'?(target?`LONG (alcista) · objetivo ${fmtUsd(target.price)} · posible short squeeze si el flujo mantiene la presión.`:'LONG (alcista) sin objetivo dominante.')
    :direction==='down'?(target?`SHORT (bajista) · objetivo ${fmtUsd(target.price)} · posible long squeeze si continúa la presión.`:'SHORT (bajista) sin objetivo dominante.')
    :'Sin confluencia independiente suficiente. Esperar confirmación.';
  return {
    direction,headline,sub,
    narrative:buildNarrative(direction,Math.round(gate*100),Math.round(contrarian*100),Math.round(momentum*100),harmony,target,lp.pullPct),
    scorePct:Math.round(drive*100),contrarianPct:Math.round(contrarian*100),momentumPct:Math.round(momentum*100),
    gatePct:Math.round(gate*100),harmony,pullPct:lp.pullPct,dominantUp:lp.up.cluster,dominantDown:lp.down.cluster,
    confidence,target,invalidation,windowH,factors,cascade,warnings,regime
  };
}

/* Historia armonizada: qué dice cada escuela y por qué manda una */
function buildNarrative(
  dir: Verdict["direction"],
  gatePct: number,
  cPct: number,
  mPct: number,
  harmony: number,
  target: Cluster | null,
  pullPct: number
): string {
  const word = (v: number) => (v >= 15 ? "alcista" : v <= -15 ? "bajista" : "neutral");
  const cw = word(cPct);
  const mw = word(mPct);
  const magnet = target
    ? target.side === "short"
      ? `el imán de shorts arriba en ${fmtUsd(target.price)} tira con ${Math.abs(pullPct)}% de fuerza`
      : `el imán de longs abajo en ${fmtUsd(target.price)} tira con ${Math.abs(pullPct)}% de fuerza`
    : "sin un imán dominante definido";
  if (dir === "up") {
    const flow = mw === "alcista" ? "y el impulso acompaña" : mw === "bajista" ? "aunque el impulso aún frena" : "con el impulso sin definir";
    return `Fase de caza (peso ${gatePct}%): ${magnet}; la lectura contrarian ${cw} es la que manda, ${flow}. Armonía ${harmony}%. El precio tiende a viajar a ese imán antes de decidir dirección.`;
  }
  if (dir === "down") {
    const flow = mw === "bajista" ? "y el impulso acompaña" : mw === "alcista" ? "aunque el impulso aún frena" : "con el impulso sin definir";
    return `Fase de caza (peso ${gatePct}%): ${magnet}; la lectura contrarian ${cw} es la que manda, ${flow}. Armonía ${harmony}%. El precio tiende a cazar esas liquidaciones antes de decidir dirección.`;
  }
  if (Math.sign(cPct) !== Math.sign(mPct) && Math.abs(cPct) >= 15 && Math.abs(mPct) >= 15) {
    return `Escuelas en desacuerdo: contrarian ${cw} vs impulso ${mw} (armonía ${harmony}%). No hay ventaja clara — espera a que un barrido defina el lado.`;
  }
  return `Ni multitud definida ni impulso claro: mercado en equilibrio (armonía ${harmony}%). El radar no tiene ventaja que ofrecerte ahora mismo.`;
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------
   Sesgo de un timeframe completo (para confluencia multi-plazo).
   Usa solo factores de acción de precio + volumen (los que se
   pueden derivar de las velas); los de datos en vivo van neutros.
   ------------------------------------------------------------ */
export interface TfBias {
  tf: string;
  label: string;
  direction: "up" | "down" | "neutral";
  scorePct: number; // -100..100
  word: string;
}

export function biasFromCandles(
  candles: Candle[],
  tf: string,
  label: string,
  msPerCandle: number,
  range: number
): TfBias {
  const spot = candles[candles.length - 1].close;
  const cvd = computeCvd(candles);
  const { longPool, shortPool, nearLongPool, nearShortPool, clusters } = estimateLiquidationClusters({
    candles,
    spot,
    leverages: [10, 25, 50, 75, 100],
    rangePct: range,
    binsCount: 72,
    oiContracts: 0,
    priceForOi: spot,
    nearPct: 2,
  });
  const n = candles.length;
  const last = spot;
  const k3 = Math.max(2, Math.floor(n / 3));
  const fastSlopePct = ((last - candles[n - 1 - k3].close) / candles[n - 1 - k3].close) * 100;
  const slowSlopePct = ((last - candles[0].close) / candles[0].close) * 100;
  const km = Math.max(2, Math.floor(n / 6));
  const momPct = ((last - candles[n - 1 - km].close) / candles[n - 1 - km].close) * 100;

  const v = computeVerdict({
    spot,
    longPool,
    shortPool,
    nearLongPool,
    nearShortPool,
    clusters,
    fundingRate: 0,
    fundingTrend: 0,
    globalRatio: 1,
    topRatio: 1,
    takerRatio: 1,
    oiChange24h: 0,
    oiSlope5m: 0,
    priceChange24h: slowSlopePct,
    premium: 0,
    atr1h: atrOf(candles) * (3600_000 / msPerCandle),
    liveLongLiq: 0,
    liveShortLiq: 0,
    cvdPct: cvd.cvdPct,
    cvdDiv: cvd.divergence,
    oiUsdt: 0,
    fastSlopePct,
    slowSlopePct,
    momPct,
    bookImbalance: 1,
    xCfundingGap: 0,
    fundingWindow: 0,
    sweep: 0,
    liqVelocity: 0,
    optionsPutCall: 1,
  });

  return {
    tf,
    label,
    direction: v.direction,
    scorePct: v.scorePct,
    word: v.direction === "up" ? "LONG" : v.direction === "down" ? "SHORT" : "NEUTRO",
  };
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------
   Imán dominante: cuánta liquidez "tira" de cada lado, ponderada
   por cercanía. Es la señal más afilada del radar — el precio
   barre primero lo que tiene cerca, no lo que es más grande lejos.
   ------------------------------------------------------------ */
export interface Magnet {
  cluster: Cluster | null; // el imán más fuerte de ese lado
  pull: number; // 0..1 — magnetismo total del lado (normalizado)
}

export interface LiquidityPull {
  pull: number; // -1..1 — positivo = tira hacia ARRIBA (hacia los shorts)
  up: Magnet; // lado short (arriba)
  down: Magnet; // lado long (abajo)
  pullPct: number; // pull redondeado en %
}

export function liquidityPull(clusters: Cluster[], atrPct: number): LiquidityPull {
  // decaimiento por distancia: lo cercano domina (nunca atrPct=0)
  const magnet = (c: Cluster) => c.estNotional * c.intensity * Math.exp(-c.distancePct / Math.max(atrPct, 0.2));

  const ups = clusters.filter((c) => c.side === "short");
  const downs = clusters.filter((c) => c.side === "long");

  const sumMag = (list: Cluster[]) => list.reduce((a, c) => a + magnet(c), 0);
  const best = (list: Cluster[]): Cluster | null =>
    list.length ? list.reduce((a, c) => (magnet(c) > magnet(a) ? c : a), list[0]) : null;

  const upMag = sumMag(ups);
  const downMag = sumMag(downs);
  const total = upMag + downMag;

  return {
    pull: total > 0 ? (upMag - downMag) / total : 0,
    up: { cluster: best(ups), pull: total > 0 ? upMag / total : 0 },
    down: { cluster: best(downs), pull: total > 0 ? downMag / total : 0 },
    pullPct: Math.round((total > 0 ? (upMag - downMag) / total : 0) * 100),
  };
}

/* Grado de confluencia entre timeframes → 0..100 */
export function confluenceGrade(biases: TfBias[]): { grade: number; label: string; alignedDir: "up" | "down" | "mixed" | null } {
  if (biases.length === 0) return { grade: 0, label: "—", alignedDir: null };
  const ups = biases.filter((b) => b.direction === "up").length;
  const downs = biases.filter((b) => b.direction === "down").length;
  const total = biases.length;
  const dominant = Math.max(ups, downs);
  const grade = Math.round((dominant / total) * 100);
  const alignedDir = ups === downs ? "mixed" : ups > downs ? "up" : "down";
  const label =
    dominant === total
      ? alignedDir === "up"
        ? "CONFLUENCIA TOTAL · LONG"
        : "CONFLUENCIA TOTAL · SHORT"
      : dominant >= total - 1
        ? `Mayoría ${alignedDir === "up" ? "LONG" : "SHORT"}`
        : "SIN ACUERDO";
  return { grade, label, alignedDir };
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------
   Formato
   ------------------------------------------------------------ */
export const fmtUsd = (v: number, digits = 0) =>
  "$" + v.toLocaleString("en-US", { maximumFractionDigits: digits, minimumFractionDigits: digits });

export const fmtCompact = (v: number) => {
  const sign = v < 0 ? "-" : "";
  const a = Math.abs(v);
  if (a >= 1e9) return sign + "$" + (a / 1e9)?.toFixed?.(2) + "B";
  if (a >= 1e6) return sign + "$" + (a / 1e6)?.toFixed?.(1) + "M";
  if (a >= 1e3) return sign + "$" + (a / 1e3)?.toFixed?.(1) + "K";
  return sign + "$" + a?.toFixed?.(0);
};

export const fmtTime = (ms: number) =>
  new Date(ms).toLocaleTimeString("es-ES", { hour: "2-digit", minute: "2-digit", second: "2-digit" });

/* ============================================================
   Radar multi-exchange: BTC en Binance vs OKX vs Bybit.
   Endpoints públicos oficiales de cada exchange (con CORS).
   ============================================================ */

export interface ExchangeQuote {
  exchange: "binance" | "okx" | "bybit";
  price: number;
  change24h: number; // %
  fundingRate: number; // fracción por periodo
  oiUsdt: number; // interés abierto nocional en USDT
  ok: boolean;
}

async function fetchJson<T>(url: string, timeoutMs = 6000): Promise<T> {
  const ctrl = new AbortController();
  const t = setTimeout(() => ctrl.abort(), timeoutMs);
  try {
    const res = await fetch(url, { signal: ctrl.signal });
    if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${res.status}`);
    return (await res.json()) as T;
  } finally {
    clearTimeout(t);
  }
}

interface BybitTicker {
  result: { list: { lastPrice: string; price24hPcnt: string; fundingRate: string; openInterestValue: string }[] };
}

export async function fetchBybit(): Promise<ExchangeQuote> {
  const r = await fetchJson<BybitTicker>("https://api.bybit.com/v5/market/tickers?category=linear&symbol=BTCUSDT");
  const t = r.result?.list?.[0];
  if (!t) throw new Error("bybit sin datos");
  return {
    exchange: "bybit",
    price: Number(t.lastPrice),
    change24h: Number(t.price24hPcnt) * 100,
    fundingRate: Number(t.fundingRate),
    oiUsdt: Number(t.openInterestValue),
    ok: true,
  };
}

interface OkxTicker {
  data: { last: string; open24h: string }[];
}
interface OkxFunding {
  data: { fundingRate: string }[];
}
interface OkxOI {
  data: { oi: string }[];
}

export async function fetchOkx(): Promise<ExchangeQuote> {
  const [tk, fd, oi] = await Promise.all([
    fetchJson<OkxTicker>("https://www.okx.com/api/v5/market/ticker?instId=BTC-USDT-SWAP"),
    fetchJson<OkxFunding>("https://www.okx.com/api/v5/public/funding-rate?instId=BTC-USDT-SWAP"),
    fetchJson<OkxOI>("https://www.okx.com/api/v5/public/open-interest?instId=BTC-USDT-SWAP"),
  ]);
  const last = Number(tk.data[0].last);
  const open = Number(tk.data[0].open24h);
  const contracts = Number(oi.data[0].oi); // contratos (ctVal = 0.01 BTC)
  return {
    exchange: "okx",
    price: last,
    change24h: open > 0 ? ((last - open) / open) * 100 : 0,
    fundingRate: Number(fd.data[0].fundingRate),
    oiUsdt: contracts * 0.01 * last,
    ok: true,
  };
}

/** Binance se alimenta de los datos en vivo que ya tiene el radar (props) */
export function binanceQuote(price: number, change24h: number, fundingRate: number, oiBtc: number): ExchangeQuote {
  return {
    exchange: "binance",
    price,
    change24h,
    fundingRate,
    oiUsdt: oiBtc > 0 ? oiBtc * price : 0,
    ok: price > 0,
  };
}

export const EXCHANGE_LABEL: Record<ExchangeQuote["exchange"], string> = {
  binance: "BINANCE",
  okx: "OKX",
  bybit: "BYBIT",
};

export const EXCHANGE_COLOR: Record<ExchangeQuote["exchange"], string> = {
  binance: "#ffb547",
  okx: "#e9f1ff",
  bybit: "#3fb6ff",
};

/* ============================================================
   Histórico de funding por exchange (para el heatmap).
   ============================================================ */
export interface FundingPoint {
  time: number; // unix ms
  rate: number; // fracción por periodo (0.0001 = 0.01%)
}

interface BinanceFunding {
  fundingRate: string;
  fundingTime: number;
}

export async function fetchFundingHistoryBinance(limit = 100): Promise<FundingPoint[]> {
  const r = await fetchJson<BinanceFunding[]>(
    `https://fapi.binance.com/fapi/v1/fundingRate?symbol=BTCUSDT&limit=${limit}`
  );
  return r.map((x) => ({ time: x.fundingTime, rate: Number(x.fundingRate) }));
}

interface OkxFundingHist {
  data: { fundingRate: string; fundingTime: string }[];
}

export async function fetchFundingHistoryOkx(limit = 100): Promise<FundingPoint[]> {
  const r = await fetchJson<OkxFundingHist>(
    `https://www.okx.com/api/v5/public/funding-rate-history?instId=BTC-USDT-SWAP&limit=${limit}`
  );
  return r.data.map((x) => ({ time: Number(x.fundingTime), rate: Number(x.fundingRate) }));
}

interface BybitFundingHist {
  result: { list: { fundingRate: string; fundingRateTimestamp: string }[] };
}

export async function fetchFundingHistoryBybit(limit = 100): Promise<FundingPoint[]> {
  const r = await fetchJson<BybitFundingHist>(
    `https://api.bybit.com/v5/market/funding/history?category=linear&symbol=BTCUSDT&limit=${limit}`
  );
  return (r.result?.list ?? []).map((x) => ({
    time: Number(x.fundingRateTimestamp),
    rate: Number(x.fundingRate),
  }));
}

FILE: src/lib/externalLiquidity.ts

export type ClusterSide = "long" | "short" | "unknown";
export type SideOrigin = "observed" | "inferred" | "unknown";

export interface ExternalCluster {
  price:number;
  side:ClusterSide;
  sideOrigin:SideOrigin;
  sideConfidence:number;
  estNotional:number;
  source:string;
  leverage?:number;
}

export async function fetchExternalClusters(symbol="BTCUSDT", currentPrice?:number):Promise<ExternalCluster[]|null>{
  const ctrl=new AbortController(); const timer=setTimeout(()=>ctrl.abort(),5000);
  try{
    const url=new URL("/api/liquidity/external", window.location.origin);
    url.searchParams.set("symbol", symbol);
    if (Number.isFinite(currentPrice)) url.searchParams.set("price", String(currentPrice));
    const r=await fetch(url,{signal:ctrl.signal,headers:{"Accept":"application/json"}});
    if(!r.ok) return null;
    const data=await r.json() as any;
    const rows=Array.isArray(data)?data:(data?.clusters??[]);
    if(!Array.isArray(rows)) return null;
    return rows.map((x:any)=>{
      const rawSide=x.side;
      const side:ClusterSide=rawSide==="short"||rawSide==="long"?rawSide:"unknown";
      const origin:SideOrigin=x.sideOrigin==="observed"||x.sideOrigin==="inferred"?x.sideOrigin:"unknown";
      return {
        price:Number(x.price),
        side,
        sideOrigin:origin,
        sideConfidence:Number.isFinite(Number(x.sideConfidence)) ? Math.max(0,Math.min(1,Number(x.sideConfidence))) : 0,
        estNotional:Number(x.estNotional??x.notional??0),
        source:String(x.source??"external"),
        leverage:Number.isFinite(Number(x.leverage))?Number(x.leverage):undefined,
      };
    }).filter((x:ExternalCluster)=>Number.isFinite(x.price)&&x.price>0&&Number.isFinite(x.estNotional)&&x.estNotional>0);
  }catch{return null} finally{clearTimeout(timer);}
}


============================================================================
FILE: src/lib/fundingHeat.ts
