import type { FundingPoint } from "./exchanges";

/* ============================================================
   Lógica del heatmap de funding por exchange.
   Alinea los settlements (cada 8h) de Binance / OKX / Bybit y
   pinta cada celda según la tasa: verde = shorts pagan,
   rojo = longs pagan. Cuanto más intenso, más multitud apilada.
   ============================================================ */

export interface FundingCell {
  time: number;
  rate: number;
}

export interface FundingRow {
  exchange: "binance" | "okx" | "bybit";
  label: string;
  color: string;
  cells: FundingCell[];
  ok: boolean;
}

export interface FundingHeatData {
  rows: FundingRow[];
  cols: number;
  maxAbs: number; // para normalizar la intensidad de color
  sim: boolean;
}

const EXCHANGES: { id: "binance" | "okx" | "bybit"; label: string; color: string }[] = [
  { id: "binance", label: "BINANCE", color: "#ffb547" },
  { id: "okx", label: "OKX", color: "#e9f1ff" },
  { id: "bybit", label: "BYBIT", color: "#3fb6ff" },
];

/** Alinea por índice desde el más reciente y recorta a `cols` columnas */
export function buildFundingHeat(
  series: Record<"binance" | "okx" | "bybit", FundingPoint[]>,
  sim: boolean,
  cols = 24
): FundingHeatData {
  const lengths = EXCHANGES.map((e) => series[e.id].length).filter((n) => n > 0);
  const n = lengths.length > 0 ? Math.min(...lengths, cols) : 0;

  let maxAbs = 0.0001;
  const rows: FundingRow[] = EXCHANGES.map((e) => {
    const pts = series[e.id];
    const cells: FundingCell[] = [];
    if (n > 0) {
      // alinear desde el final (los más recientes)
      const slice = pts.slice(pts.length - n);
      for (const p of slice) {
        cells.push({ time: p.time, rate: p.rate });
        maxAbs = Math.max(maxAbs, Math.abs(p.rate));
      }
    }
    return { exchange: e.id, label: e.label, color: e.color, cells, ok: cells.length > 0 };
  });

  return { rows, cols: n, maxAbs, sim };
}

/** Escala de color: verde (shorts pagan) → neutro → rojo (longs pagan) */
export function rateColor(rate: number, maxAbs: number): string {
  const base = 0.0001; // funding "neutro" de referencia
  const t = Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, (rate - base) / Math.max(maxAbs - base, 0.00005)));
  // t < 0 → verde, t > 0 → rojo
  const a = 0.18 + 0.72 * Math.abs(t); // opacidad según intensidad
  if (t >= 0) {
    return `rgba(255,77,109,${a?.toFixed?.(2)})`;
  }
  return `rgba(47,214,165,${a?.toFixed?.(2)})`;
}

/** Quién paga en cada celda */
export function payer(rate: number): string {
  if (rate > 0.00012) return "longs pagan";
  if (rate < 0.00008) return "shorts pagan";
  return "equilibrado";
}

export function fmtRate(rate: number): string {
  return (rate * 100)?.toFixed?.(4) + "%";
}

/* Simulador coherente para cuando no hay red */


============================================================================
FILE: src/lib/heatmap.ts
============================================================================

/* ============================================================
   Heatmap 2D de liquidación (tiempo × precio) — estilo Coinglass
   Cada columna temporal es un mapa de liquidación estimado con
   las velas anteriores (ventana deslizante), sin mirar al futuro.
   ============================================================ */

import type { Candle } from "./engine";
import { liqDistance } from "./engine";

export interface HeatmapData {
  cols: number;
  bins: number;
  matrix: Float32Array; // cols * bins, fila 0 = precio máximo (arriba)
  closes: number[];
  times: number[]; // unix seconds por columna
  priceMin: number;
  priceMax: number;
}

export function buildHeatmap(
  candles: Candle[],
  leverages: number[],
  bins = 64,
  lookback = 24
): HeatmapData {
  const n = candles.length;
  let lo = Infinity;
  let hi = -Infinity;
  for (const c of candles) {
    if (c.low < lo) lo = c.low;
    if (c.high > hi) hi = c.high;
  }
  const pad = (hi - lo) * 0.035 + hi * 0.002;
  const priceMin = lo - pad;
  const priceMax = hi + pad;
  const step = (priceMax - priceMin) / bins;
  const inv = 1 / step;

  const raw = new Float32Array(n * bins);

  const add = (arr: Float32Array, price: number, w: number) => {
    const idx = (priceMax - price) * inv;
    const i0 = Math.floor(idx);
    for (let o = -1; o <= 1; o++) {
      const b = i0 + o;
      if (b < 0 || b >= bins) continue;
      arr[b] += w * (o === 0 ? 1 : 0.42);
    }
  };

  const levWeight: Record<number, number> = { 10: 1, 25: 0.85, 50: 0.68, 100: 0.5 };
  const tmp = new Float32Array(bins);

  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    tmp.fill(0);
    const start = Math.max(0, i - lookback + 1);
    for (let j = start; j <= i; j++) {
      const c = candles[j];
      const recency = Math.exp(-(i - j) / (lookback * 0.55));
      const volW = Math.pow(c.quoteVolume, 0.35);
      const base = recency * volW;
      for (const L of leverages) {
        const d = 1 / Math.max(L,1);
        const lw = levWeight[L] ?? 0.7;
        add(tmp, c.high * (1 - d), base * 0.9 * lw);
        add(tmp, c.close * (1 - d), base * 0.45 * lw);
        add(tmp, c.low * (1 + d), base * 0.9 * lw);
        add(tmp, c.close * (1 + d), base * 0.45 * lw);
      }
    }
    // suavizado vertical ligero
    const col = raw.subarray(i * bins, i * bins + bins);
    for (let b = 0; b < bins; b++) {
      const a = tmp[Math.max(0, b - 1)];
      const m = tmp[b];
      const c2 = tmp[Math.min(bins - 1, b + 1)];
      col[b] = (a + 2 * m + c2) / 4;
    }
  }

  // normalización global para color consistente
  let gmax = 1e-9;
  for (let k = 0; k < raw.length; k++) if (raw[k] > gmax) gmax = raw[k];
  const matrix = new Float32Array(raw.length);
  for (let k = 0; k < raw.length; k++) matrix[k] = raw[k] / gmax;

  return {
    cols: n,
    bins,
    matrix,
    closes: candles.map((c) => c.close),
    times: candles.map((c) => c.time),
    priceMin,
    priceMax,
  };
}

/* Color por celda: lado según la posición respecto al cierre de esa columna.
   Debajo → verdes (liq longs) · Encima → rojos (liq shorts) · brillo = intensidad */
export function heatColor(intensity: number, below: boolean): string {
  const t = Math.pow(Math.max(0, Math.min(1, intensity)), 0.62);
  if (t <= 0.015) return below ? "rgba(23,52,46,0.55)" : "rgba(58,26,36,0.55)";
  let r: number, g: number, b: number, a: number;
  if (below) {
    // teal oscuro → verde → verde-amarillo caliente
    r = Math.round(16 + t * t * 190);
    g = Math.round(58 + t * 190);
    b = Math.round(52 + t * 90);
    a = 0.28 + t * 0.72;
  } else {
    // granate → rojo → naranja-amarillo caliente
    r = Math.round(70 + t * 185);
    g = Math.round(22 + t * t * 150);
    b = Math.round(40 + t * 40);
    a = 0.28 + t * 0.72;
  }
  return `rgba(${r},${g},${b},${a?.toFixed?.(3)})`;
}


============================================================================
FILE: src/lib/history.ts
============================================================================

/* ============================================================
   Track record del modelo: registra cada veredicto con sesgo y
   lo evalúa contra el precio real (objetivo vs invalidación)
   ============================================================ */

import type { Verdict } from "./engine";

export type PredStatus = "abierta" | "acierto" | "fallo" | "caducada";

export interface Prediction {
  id: string;
  time: number;
  direction: "up" | "down";
  headline: string;
  spot: number;
  target: number | null;
  invalidation: number | null;
  scorePct: number;
  confidence: number;
  windowH: [number, number];
  status: PredStatus;
  resolvedAt?: number;
  note?: string;
}

const KEY = "liqradar-preds-v2";
const MAX_STORED = 40;

export function loadPredictions(): Prediction[] {
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(KEY);
    if (!raw) return [];
    const list = JSON.parse(raw) as Prediction[];
    return Array.isArray(list) ? list.slice(0, MAX_STORED) : [];
  } catch {
    return [];
  }
}

export function savePredictions(list: Prediction[]) {
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(KEY, JSON.stringify(list.slice(0, MAX_STORED)));
  } catch {
    /* almacenamiento no disponible */
  }
}

export function evaluatePredictions(list: Prediction[], spot: number, now: number): { list: Prediction[]; changed: boolean } {
  let changed = false;
  const out = list.map((p) => {
    if (p.status !== "abierta") return p;
    const ageH = (now - p.time) / 3600_000;

    if (p.direction === "up") {
      if (p.target !== null && spot >= p.target) {
        changed = true;
        return { ...p, status: "acierto" as const, resolvedAt: now, note: "Objetivo de shorts alcanzado" };
      }
      if (p.invalidation !== null && spot <= p.invalidation) {
        changed = true;
        return { ...p, status: "fallo" as const, resolvedAt: now, note: "Invalidación barrida antes" };
      }
    } else {
      if (p.target !== null && spot <= p.target) {
        changed = true;
        return { ...p, status: "acierto" as const, resolvedAt: now, note: "Objetivo de longs alcanzado" };
      }
      if (p.invalidation !== null && spot >= p.invalidation) {
        changed = true;
        return { ...p, status: "fallo" as const, resolvedAt: now, note: "Invalidación barrida antes" };
      }
    }
    if (ageH > Math.max(24, p.windowH[1] * 1.5)) {
      changed = true;
      return { ...p, status: "caducada" as const, resolvedAt: now, note: "Ventana superada sin resolución" };
    }
    return p;
  });
  return { list: out, changed };
}

/** ¿Vale la pena registrar este veredicto? Evita duplicados por el mismo escenario. */
export function shouldRecord(v: Verdict, spot: number, last: Prediction | null, now: number): boolean {
  if (v.direction === "neutral" || !v.target) return false;
  const key = `${v.direction}:${Math.round(v.target.price / 200)}`;
  if (!last) return true;
  const lastKey = `${last.direction}:${last.target !== null ? Math.round(last.target / 200) : ""}`;
  if (key !== lastKey) return now - last.time > 4 * 60_000; // cambio de escenario: mínimo 4 min
  return now - last.time > 20 * 60_000; // mismo escenario: refrescar cada 20 min
}

export function toPrediction(v: Verdict, spot: number, now: number): Prediction {
  return {
    id: `p-${now}-${Math.floor(Math.random() * 1e6)}`,
    time: now,
    direction: v.direction as "up" | "down",
    headline: v.headline,
    spot,
    target: v.target?.price ?? null,
    invalidation: v.invalidation?.price ?? null,
    scorePct: v.scorePct,
    confidence: v.confidence,
    windowH: v.windowH,
    status: "abierta",
  };
}

export interface TrackStats {
  hits: number;
  misses: number;
  open: number;
  expired: number;
  hitRate: number | null; // null = sin datos cerrados
  avgConfOnHit: number | null;
}

export function trackStats(list: Prediction[]): TrackStats {
  const hits = list.filter((p) => p.status === "acierto").length;
  const misses = list.filter((p) => p.status === "fallo").length;
  const open = list.filter((p) => p.status === "abierta").length;
  const expired = list.filter((p) => p.status === "caducada").length;
  const closed = hits + misses;
  const hitList = list.filter((p) => p.status === "acierto");
  return {
    hits,
    misses,
    open,
    expired,
    hitRate: closed > 0 ? (hits / closed) * 100 : null,
    avgConfOnHit: hitList.length > 0 ? hitList.reduce((a, p) => a + p.confidence, 0) / hitList.length : null,
  };
}


============================================================================
FILE: src/lib/l2History.ts
============================================================================

export interface L2LevelPoint {
  price: number;
  notional: number;
}

export interface L2Snapshot {
  time: number;
  mid: number;
  bids: L2LevelPoint[];
  asks: L2LevelPoint[];
}

export interface L2HeatmapData {
  cols: number;
  bins: number;
  matrix: Float32Array;
  mids: number[];
  times: number[];
  priceMin: number;
  priceMax: number;
}

const STORAGE_KEY = "liqradar:l2-history:v1";
const MAX_SNAPSHOTS = 900; // ~15 min at 1 sample/s
const PERSIST_EVERY = 20;
let persistCounter = 0;

function trimLevel(level: L2LevelPoint): L2LevelPoint {
  return { price: Number(level.price), notional: Math.max(0, Number(level.notional)) };
}

export function normalizeL2Snapshot(snapshot: L2Snapshot): L2Snapshot {
  return {
    time: Number(snapshot.time),
    mid: Number(snapshot.mid),
    bids: snapshot.bids.map(trimLevel).filter((x) => Number.isFinite(x.price) && Number.isFinite(x.notional) && x.notional > 0),
    asks: snapshot.asks.map(trimLevel).filter((x) => Number.isFinite(x.price) && Number.isFinite(x.notional) && x.notional > 0),
  };
}

export function appendL2Snapshot(history: L2Snapshot[], snapshot: L2Snapshot): L2Snapshot[] {
  const next = [...history, normalizeL2Snapshot(snapshot)];
  return next.length > MAX_SNAPSHOTS ? next.slice(next.length - MAX_SNAPSHOTS) : next;
}

interface StoredSnapshot { t:number; m:number; b:number[]; a:number[]; }

function compactSnapshot(s:L2Snapshot):StoredSnapshot{
  const flatten=(rows:L2LevelPoint[])=>rows.flatMap(x=>[x.price,x.notional]);
  return {t:s.time,m:s.mid,b:flatten(s.bids),a:flatten(s.asks)};
}
function expandSnapshot(s:StoredSnapshot):L2Snapshot{
  const expand=(rows:number[])=>{const out:L2LevelPoint[]=[];for(let i=0;i+1<rows.length;i+=2){const price=Number(rows[i]),notional=Number(rows[i+1]);if(Number.isFinite(price)&&price>0&&Number.isFinite(notional)&&notional>0)out.push({price,notional});}return out;};
  return {time:Number(s.t),mid:Number(s.m),bids:expand(s.b),asks:expand(s.a)};
}

export function loadL2History(): L2Snapshot[] {
  if (typeof window === "undefined") return [];
  try {
    const raw = window.localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);
    if (!raw) return [];
    const parsed = JSON.parse(raw) as StoredSnapshot[];
    if (!Array.isArray(parsed)) return [];
    return parsed.slice(-MAX_SNAPSHOTS).map(expandSnapshot).filter((x) => x.mid > 0 && x.time > 0);
  } catch {
    return [];
  }
}

export function persistL2History(history: L2Snapshot[], force = false): void {
  if (typeof window === "undefined") return;
  persistCounter += 1;
  if (!force && persistCounter % PERSIST_EVERY !== 0) return;
  try {
    const compact=history.slice(-MAX_SNAPSHOTS).map(compactSnapshot);
    window.localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(compact));
  } catch {
    try {
      // Preserve recent data if browser quota is tight.
      const compact=history.slice(-300).map(compactSnapshot);
      window.localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(compact));
    } catch {
      // Live L2 remains active; persistence is optional.
    }
  }
}

function finiteBounds(history: L2Snapshot[]) {
  let lo = Infinity;
  let hi = -Infinity;
  for (const s of history) {
    lo = Math.min(lo, s.mid);
    hi = Math.max(hi, s.mid);
    for (const x of s.bids) lo = Math.min(lo, x.price);
    for (const x of s.asks) hi = Math.max(hi, x.price);
  }
  if (!Number.isFinite(lo) || !Number.isFinite(hi) || lo <= 0 || hi <= 0) return null;
  const span = Math.max(hi - lo, hi * 0.0025);
  const pad = span * 0.03;
  return { priceMin: Math.max(0, lo - pad), priceMax: hi + pad };
}

/**
 * Builds a real historical L2 heatmap from the snapshots captured by the app.
 * It never invents liquidity levels. Intensity is log-scaled from observed resting notional.
 */
export function buildL2Heatmap(history: L2Snapshot[], bins = 96): L2HeatmapData | null {
  if (history.length < 4) return null;
  const bounds = finiteBounds(history);
  if (!bounds) return null;
  const { priceMin, priceMax } = bounds;
  const step = (priceMax - priceMin) / bins;
  const raw = new Float32Array(history.length * bins);
  let globalMax = 0;

  const add = (col: number, price: number, notional: number) => {
    const idx = Math.floor((price - priceMin) / step);
    if (idx < 0 || idx >= bins) return;
    const weight = Math.log1p(Math.max(0, notional));
    raw[col * bins + idx] += weight;
    if (raw[col * bins + idx] > globalMax) globalMax = raw[col * bins + idx];
  };

  for (let c = 0; c < history.length; c += 1) {
    const s = history[c];
    for (const x of s.bids) add(c, x.price, x.notional);
    for (const x of s.asks) add(c, x.price, x.notional);
  }

  const matrix = new Float32Array(raw.length);
  if (globalMax > 0) {
    for (let i = 0; i < raw.length; i += 1) matrix[i] = raw[i] / globalMax;
  }

  return {
    cols: history.length,
    bins,
    matrix,
    mids: history.map((x) => x.mid),
    times: history.map((x) => x.time),
    priceMin,
    priceMax,
  };
}


============================================================================
FILE: src/lib/levels.ts
============================================================================

/* ============================================================
   Niveles clave y estructura — niveles objetivos derivados de
   las velas (sin estimación): apertura diaria/semanal, máx/mín
   del día anterior, rango visible, Fibonacci y pivotes clásicos.
   ============================================================ */

import type { Candle } from "./engine";

export type LevelKind = "resistencia" | "soporte" | "neutro";

export interface KeyLevel {
  price: number;
  label: string;
  short: string;
  kind: LevelKind; // relativo al spot actual
  group: "estructura" | "fib" | "pivote";
  distancePct: number; // (price − spot)/spot ×100
}

const FIBS = [
  { r: 0.236, s: "23.6" },
  { r: 0.382, s: "38.2" },
  { r: 0.5, s: "50.0" },
  { r: 0.618, s: "61.8" },
  { r: 0.786, s: "78.6" },
];

/** día UTC de la semana: 0=domingo … 6=sábado (getUTCDay) */
export function computeKeyLevels(daily: Candle[], visible: Candle[], spot: number): KeyLevel[] {
  if (!Number.isFinite(spot) || spot <= 0) return [];
  const out: { price: number; label: string; short: string; group: KeyLevel["group"] }[] = [];
  const dist = (p: number) => ((p - spot) / spot) * 100;

  /* --- estructura diaria/semanal (de velas 1d) --- */
  if (daily.length >= 2) {
    const prev = daily[daily.length - 2]; // día anterior cerrado
    const today = daily[daily.length - 1]; // día en curso
    out.push({ price: prev.high, label: "Máximo día anterior", short: "PDH", group: "estructura" });
    out.push({ price: prev.low, label: "Mínimo día anterior", short: "PDL", group: "estructura" });
    out.push({ price: today.open, label: "Apertura diaria (00:00 UTC)", short: "DO", group: "estructura" });

    // apertura semanal: vela del lunes (getUTCDay === 1) más reciente
    let monday: Candle | null = null;
    for (let i = daily.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      if (new Date(daily[i].time * 1000).getUTCDay() === 1) {
        monday = daily[i];
        break;
      }
    }
    if (monday) out.push({ price: monday.open, label: "Apertura semanal (lunes)", short: "WO", group: "estructura" });
  }

  /* --- rango visible + Fibonacci --- */
  if (visible.length >= 2) {
    let hi = -Infinity;
    let lo = Infinity;
    for (const c of visible) {
      if (c.high > hi) hi = c.high;
      if (c.low < lo) lo = c.low;
    }
    out.push({ price: hi, label: "Máximo del rango visible", short: "RH", group: "estructura" });
    out.push({ price: lo, label: "Mínimo del rango visible", short: "RL", group: "estructura" });
    for (const f of FIBS) {
      const p = hi - (hi - lo) * f.r; // retroceso desde el máximo
      out.push({ price: p, label: `Fibonacci ${f.s}%`, short: `F${f.s}`, group: "fib" });
    }
  }

  /* --- pivotes clásicos del día anterior --- */
  if (daily.length >= 2) {
    const prev = daily[daily.length - 2];
    const P = (prev.high + prev.low + prev.close) / 3;
    out.push({ price: P, label: "Pivote diario", short: "P", group: "pivote" });
    out.push({ price: 2 * P - prev.low, label: "Resistencia 1", short: "R1", group: "pivote" });
    out.push({ price: P + (prev.high - prev.low), label: "Resistencia 2", short: "R2", group: "pivote" });
    out.push({ price: 2 * P - prev.high, label: "Soporte 1", short: "S1", group: "pivote" });
    out.push({ price: P - (prev.high - prev.low), label: "Soporte 2", short: "S2", group: "pivote" });
  }

  // dedup por precio cercano y clasifica contra el spot
  const seen = new Map<number, KeyLevel>();
  for (const l of out) {
    if (!Number.isFinite(l.price) || l.price <= 0) continue;
    const key = Math.round(l.price / (spot * 0.0004)); // agrupa niveles a <0.04%
    if (seen.has(key)) continue;
    const kind: LevelKind = Math.abs(l.price - spot) / spot < 0.0015 ? "neutro" : l.price > spot ? "resistencia" : "soporte";
    seen.set(key, { ...l, kind, distancePct: dist(l.price) });
  }
  return Array.from(seen.values()).sort((a, b) => b.price - a.price);
}

export const KIND_COLOR: Record<LevelKind, string> = {
  resistencia: "#ff4d6d",
  soporte: "#2fd6a5",
  neutro: "#93a5c8",
};


============================================================================
FILE: src/lib/liquidationModel.ts
============================================================================

import type { Candle, Cluster, LevPart, LiqBin } from './engine';

export interface BinanceBracket {
  bracket: number;
  initialLeverage: number;
  notionalFloor: number;
  notionalCap: number;
  maintMarginRatio: number;
  cum: number;
}

export interface LiquidationModelInput {
  candles: Candle[];
  spot: number;
  leverages: number[];
  rangePct: number;
  binsCount?: number;
  oiContracts: number;
  priceForOi?: number;
  globalLongShort?: number;
  topLongShort?: number;
  brackets?: BinanceBracket[];
  recentLiquidations?: { side: 'long' | 'short'; price: number; notional: number; time: number }[];
  nearPct?: number;
}

export interface LiquidationModelOutput {
  bins: LiqBin[];
  longPool: number;
  shortPool: number;
  nearLongPool: number;
  nearShortPool: number;
  clusters: Cluster[];
  dataQuality: 'estimated' | 'bracket-assisted' | 'high-confidence-estimate';
  caveat: string;
}

const clamp01 = (x: number) => Math.min(1, Math.max(0, x));

/**
 * Binance liquidation is driven by mark price, entry price, position size,
 * leverage/margin and the maintenance-margin bracket. Public market data does
 * not expose all traders' entry prices or leverage, so future clusters can
 * only be estimated. This model uses REAL OI + REAL long/short ratios + REAL
 * bracket tiers (when configured) and never fabricates liquidation events.
 */
export function liquidationDistanceFromBracket(
  entry: number,
  leverage: number,
  bracket: BinanceBracket | undefined,
  side: 'long' | 'short'
): number {
  const mmr = bracket?.maintMarginRatio ?? Math.max(0.004, 0.0065 - Math.min(0.0025, leverage * 0.00002));
  // The Binance cumulative bracket term is position-size dependent. Public
  // market data does not expose each trader's position size/margin, so we do
  // not invent that adjustment. The real bracket MMR anchors this estimate.
  const offset = Math.max(0.0005, 1 / Math.max(leverage, 1) - mmr);
  return side === 'long'
    ? Math.max(1, entry * (1 - offset))
    : Math.max(1, entry * (1 + offset));
}

function bracketForNotional(brackets: BinanceBracket[] | undefined, notional: number): BinanceBracket | undefined {
  if (!brackets?.length) return undefined;
  return [...brackets].sort((a, b) => a.notionalFloor - b.notionalFloor)
    .find((b) => notional >= b.notionalFloor && notional <= b.notionalCap) ??
    [...brackets].sort((a, b) => b.notionalFloor - a.notionalFloor)[0];
}

function quantile(xs: number[], q: number): number {
  if (!xs.length) return 0;
  const a = [...xs].sort((x, y) => x - y);
  const i = (a.length - 1) * q;
  const lo = Math.floor(i), hi = Math.ceil(i);
  return lo === hi ? a[lo] : a[lo] + (a[hi] - a[lo]) * (i - lo);
}

export function estimateLiquidationClusters(input: LiquidationModelInput): LiquidationModelOutput {
  const {
    candles,
    spot,
    leverages,
    rangePct,
    binsCount = 72,
    oiContracts,
    priceForOi = spot,
    globalLongShort = 1,
    topLongShort = 1,
    brackets,
    recentLiquidations = [],
    nearPct = 2,
  } = input;

  const hi = spot * (1 + rangePct);
  const lo = spot * (1 - rangePct);
  const step = Math.max((hi - lo) / binsCount, spot * 0.00005);
  const rawLong = new Float64Array(binsCount);
  const rawShort = new Float64Array(binsCount);
  const levRawLong: Record<number, Float64Array> = {};
  const levRawShort: Record<number, Float64Array> = {};
  for (const l of leverages) {
    levRawLong[l] = new Float64Array(binsCount);
    levRawShort[l] = new Float64Array(binsCount);
  }

  const totalVolume = candles.reduce((s, c) => s + Math.max(0, c.quoteVolume), 0) || 1;
  const closes = candles.map(c => c.close).filter(Number.isFinite);
  const median = quantile(closes, 0.5) || spot;
  const q25 = quantile(closes, 0.25) || median;
  const q75 = quantile(closes, 0.75) || median;
  const entryCenter = clamp01(0.35 + Math.abs(spot - median) / Math.max(spot * rangePct, 1) * 0.25);

  // Public ratios describe account/position skew, not the full notional book.
  // We use them only to split total OI into an estimated long/short notional.
  const rGlobal = Number.isFinite(globalLongShort) && globalLongShort > 0 ? globalLongShort : 1;
  const rTop = Number.isFinite(topLongShort) && topLongShort > 0 ? topLongShort : 1;
  const ratio = 0.35 * rGlobal + 0.65 * rTop;
  const longShare = ratio / (1 + ratio);
  const shortShare = 1 - longShare;
  const oiNotional = Math.max(0, oiContracts) * priceForOi;

  // Only a conservative fraction of OI is assigned to the visible range because
  // public OI does not reveal entry-price density. This avoids pretending that
  // 100% of OI is liquidatable inside the displayed window.
  const visibleFraction = Math.max(0.45, Math.min(0.70, 0.45 + rangePct * 1.8));
  const modelPool = oiNotional > 0 ? oiNotional * visibleFraction : 0;
  const longPoolTarget = modelPool * longShare;
  const shortPoolTarget = modelPool * shortShare;

  // Recent trading activity provides a proxy for likely entry-price density.
  // This does NOT identify individual positions.
  const add = (arr: Float64Array, levArr: Float64Array, price: number, weight: number) => {
    if (!Number.isFinite(price) || price < lo || price > hi || weight <= 0) return;
    const idx = (price - lo) / step;
    const base = Math.floor(idx);
    for (let d = -2; d <= 2; d++) {
      const i = base + d;
      if (i < 0 || i >= binsCount) continue;
      const k = d === 0 ? 1 : 0.32;
      arr[i] += weight * k;
      levArr[i] += weight * k;
    }
  };

  const levPrior = (lev: number) => {
    const known = [10, 25, 50, 75, 100, 125, 150, 200];
    const nearest = known.reduce((b, x) => Math.abs(x - lev) < Math.abs(b - lev) ? x : b, known[0]);
    return ({ 10: 1, 25: 0.86, 50: 0.68, 75: 0.56, 100: 0.45, 125: 0.35, 150: 0.25, 200: 0.18 } as Record<number, number>)[nearest] ?? 0.4;
  };

  for (let i = 0; i < candles.length; i++) {
    const c = candles[i];
    const age = candles.length - 1 - i;
    const recency = Math.exp(-age / Math.max(10, candles.length * 0.45));
    const volW = Math.sqrt(Math.max(c.quoteVolume, 1) / totalVolume);
    const entryWeight = recency * volW;
    const upBias = clamp01((c.close - q25) / Math.max(q75 - q25, spot * 0.002));
    const longEntry = c.close * (0.72 + 0.22 * upBias + 0.06 * entryCenter);
    const shortEntry = c.close * (1.18 - 0.18 * upBias + 0.04 * (1 - entryCenter));

    const priorSum = leverages.reduce((s,L)=>s+levPrior(L),0)||1;
    for (const L of leverages) {
      const levyWeight=levPrior(L)/priorSum;
      const estSliceNotional = Math.max(1, modelPool * levyWeight);
      const b = bracketForNotional(brackets, estSliceNotional);
      const liqLong = liquidationDistanceFromBracket(longEntry, L, b, 'long');
      const liqShort = liquidationDistanceFromBracket(shortEntry, L, b, 'short');
      const w = entryWeight * levPrior(L);
      add(rawLong, levRawLong[L], liqLong, w * longShare);
      add(rawShort, levRawShort[L], liqShort, w * shortShare);
    }
  }

  // Real executed liquidation events add local evidence. They never become
  // “future positions”, but they sharpen clusters around where liquidations
  // have actually printed.
  for (const ev of recentLiquidations.slice(0, 200)) {
    const ageMs = Date.now() - ev.time;
    const freshness = ageMs <= 15 * 60_000 ? 1 : Math.exp(-ageMs / (2 * 3600_000));
    const eventW = freshness * Math.log10(Math.max(ev.notional, 100)) * 0.15;
    if (ev.side === 'long') add(rawLong, levRawLong[leverages[0]], ev.price, eventW);
    else add(rawShort, levRawShort[leverages[0]], ev.price, eventW);
  }

  const smooth = (src: Float64Array) => {
    const out = new Float64Array(src.length);
    for (let i = 0; i < src.length; i++) out[i] = (src[Math.max(0, i - 1)] + 2 * src[i] + src[Math.min(src.length - 1, i + 1)]) / 4;
    return out;
  };

  const sLong = smooth(rawLong);
  const sShort = smooth(rawShort);
  const sLevLong: Record<number, Float64Array> = {};
  const sLevShort: Record<number, Float64Array> = {};
  for (const L of leverages) {
    sLevLong[L] = smooth(levRawLong[L]);
    sLevShort[L] = smooth(levRawShort[L]);
  }

  const sumLong = Array.from(sLong).reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) || 1;
  const sumShort = Array.from(sShort).reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) || 1;
  const max = Math.max(...Array.from(sLong), ...Array.from(sShort), 1e-9);
  const longScale = longPoolTarget > 0 ? longPoolTarget / sumLong : 0;
  const shortScale = shortPoolTarget > 0 ? shortPoolTarget / sumShort : 0;

  const bins: LiqBin[] = [];
  let nearLongPool = 0;
  let nearShortPool = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < binsCount; i++) {
    const price = lo + (i + 0.5) * step;
    const isLong = price < spot;
    const raw = isLong ? sLong[i] : sShort[i];
    const pool = isLong ? raw * longScale : raw * shortScale;
    const near = Math.abs((price - spot) / spot) * 100 <= nearPct;
    if (isLong) nearLongPool += pool; else nearShortPool += pool;
    const parts: LevPart[] = [];
    for (const L of leverages) {
      const lv = isLong ? sLevLong[L][i] : sLevShort[L][i];
      if (lv > max * 0.01) parts.push({ lev: L, v: lv / max });
    }
    bins.push({ price, intensity: raw / max, side: isLong ? 'long' : 'short', estNotional: pool, parts });
  }

  const all = bins.map((b, i) => ({ i, v: b.intensity })).filter(x => x.v >= 0.20);
  const peaks: { i: number; v: number }[] = [];
  for (const p of all) {
    if (p.i === 0 || p.i === binsCount - 1) continue;
    if (p.v >= bins[p.i - 1].intensity && p.v >= bins[p.i + 1].intensity) {
      const last = peaks[peaks.length - 1];
      if (!last || p.i - last.i >= 4) peaks.push(p);
      else if (p.v > last.v) peaks[peaks.length - 1] = p;
    }
  }

  const top = peaks.sort((a, b) => b.v - a.v).slice(0, 10);
  let longTag = 0, shortTag = 0;
  const clusters = top.map(({ i }) => {
    const b = bins[i];
    const tag = b.side === 'long' ? `L${++longTag}` : `S${++shortTag}`;
    return {
      price: b.price,
      intensity: b.intensity,
      side: b.side,
      estNotional: b.estNotional,
      distancePct: Math.abs((b.price - spot) / spot) * 100,
      tag,
    } satisfies Cluster;
  }).sort((a, b) => (b.estNotional * b.intensity) - (a.estNotional * a.intensity));

  const longPool = bins.filter(b => b.side === 'long').reduce((s, b) => s + b.estNotional, 0);
  const shortPool = bins.filter(b => b.side === 'short').reduce((s, b) => s + b.estNotional, 0);
  const dataQuality = brackets?.length ?
    (recentLiquidations.length ? 'high-confidence-estimate' : 'bracket-assisted') : 'estimated';
  const caveat = brackets?.length
    ? 'Clusters estimados con OI/ratios públicos + brackets de mantenimiento reales. Binance no publica las entradas/apalancamientos individuales de todo el mercado.'
    : 'Clusters estimados con OI/ratios públicos. Configura el endpoint firmado de brackets para usar MMR/caps reales por nivel.';

  return { bins, longPool, shortPool, nearLongPool, nearShortPool, clusters, dataQuality, caveat };
}


============================================================================
FILE: src/lib/microBacktest.ts
============================================================================

import type { Candle, Verdict } from './engine';
import type { L2Snapshot } from './l2History';
import type { FlowTrade } from './microstructure';
import { detectAdvancedAbsorption, computeTradeCvd, detectRealSweep, buildVolumeProfile } from './microstructure';
import { liquidationBurst } from './microstructure';

export interface MicroReplayInput {
  candles: Candle[];
  l2: L2Snapshot[];
  futuresTrades: FlowTrade[];
  liquidations: Array<{time:number;notional:number;side:'long'|'short'}>;
}

export interface MicroReplayResult {
  samples: number;
  coverageMinutes: number;
  l2Samples: number;
  tradeSamples: number;
  liquidationSamples: number;
  absorptionEvents: number;
  sweepEvents: number;
  burstEvents: number;
  longBiasEvents: number;
  shortBiasEvents: number;
  neutralEvents: number;
  avgAbsorption: number;
  avgCvdPct: number;
  note: string;
}

function closestCandle(candles:Candle[], t:number):Candle|null {
  if(!candles.length) return null;
  let best=candles[0], d=Math.abs(best.time-t);
  for(const c of candles){ const x=Math.abs(c.time-t); if(x<d){best=c;d=x;} }
  return d<=2*3600 ? best : null;
}

/**
 * Replays only microstructure that was actually captured by LIQRADAR.
 * It is not a fabricated historical backtest. No L2/trade data is synthesized.
 */
export function runMicrostructureReplay(input:MicroReplayInput):MicroReplayResult {
  const times=input.l2.map(x=>x.time).sort((a,b)=>a-b);
  const start=times[0]??0, end=times.at(-1)??0;
  const coverageMinutes=start&&end?Math.max(0,(end-start)/60000):0;
  if(input.l2.length<4 || input.futuresTrades.length<40){
    return {samples:0,coverageMinutes,l2Samples:input.l2.length,tradeSamples:input.futuresTrades.length,liquidationSamples:input.liquidations.length,absorptionEvents:0,sweepEvents:0,burstEvents:0,longBiasEvents:0,shortBiasEvents:0,neutralEvents:0,avgAbsorption:0,avgCvdPct:0,note:'Cobertura insuficiente: acumula más L2/trades reales antes de validar microestructura.'};
  }
  let absN=0,sweepN=0,burstN=0,longN=0,shortN=0,neutralN=0,absSum=0,cvdSum=0,samples=0;
  const step=Math.max(1,Math.floor(input.l2.length/120));
  for(let i=3;i<input.l2.length;i+=step){
    const snap=input.l2[i], candle=closestCandle(input.candles,snap.time);
    const trades=input.futuresTrades.filter(t=>Math.abs(t.time-snap.time)<=90_000);
    if(!candle || trades.length<20) continue;
    const candles=input.candles.filter(c=>c.time<=snap.time).slice(-6);
    const abs=detectAdvancedAbsorption(trades,candles,input.l2.slice(Math.max(0,i-7),i+1),snap.time);
    const cvd=computeTradeCvd(trades);
    const sweep=detectRealSweep(candles,Math.min(8,Math.max(2,candles.length-2)));
    const liq=input.liquidations.filter(x=>Math.abs(x.time-snap.time)<=300_000);
    const burst=liquidationBurst(liq.map(x=>({time:x.time,notional:x.notional})),snap.time);
    samples++; absSum+=abs.score; cvdSum+=cvd.pct;
    if(Math.abs(abs.score)>=0.35) absN++;
    if(sweep!==0) sweepN++;
    if(burst.ratio>=3) burstN++;
    const bias=abs.score + cvd.pct + sweep*0.25;
    if(bias>0.25) longN++; else if(bias<-0.25) shortN++; else neutralN++;
  }
  const vp=buildVolumeProfile(input.candles.slice(-72));
  void vp;
  return {samples,coverageMinutes,l2Samples:input.l2.length,tradeSamples:input.futuresTrades.length,liquidationSamples:input.liquidations.length,absorptionEvents:absN,sweepEvents:sweepN,burstEvents:burstN,longBiasEvents:longN,shortBiasEvents:shortN,neutralEvents:neutralN,avgAbsorption:samples?absSum/samples:0,avgCvdPct:samples?cvdSum/samples:0,note:'Replay de microestructura observado. La muestra solo es válida para el intervalo realmente capturado; no sustituye un histórico L2 inexistente.'};
}


============================================================================
FILE: src/lib/microstructure.ts
============================================================================

import type { Candle } from "./engine";
import type { OrderBook } from "./binance";
import type { L2Snapshot } from "./l2History";

export interface FlowTrade {
  time: number;
  price: number;
  qty: number;
  notional: number;
  isBuyerMaker: boolean;
}
export interface BookSnapshotLite {
  time:number; bidNotional:number; askNotional:number; imbalance:number; mid:number; spreadBps:number;
}
export interface MicrostructureSnapshot {
  snapshot: BookSnapshotLite;
  bookDelta:number;
  bookPersistence:number;
  liquidityAdded:number;
  liquidityRemoved:number;
  addRemoveImbalance:number;
  absorptionScore:number;
  spoofRisk:number;
  sweep:number;
  volumeProfile:Array<{price:number;volume:number}>;
  poc:number;
  vwap:number;
}
const clamp=(x:number,a=-1,b=1)=>Math.max(a,Math.min(b,x));


/** Backwards-compatible candle CVD. Uses exchange-provided takerBuyQuote when available. */
export function computeCvd(candles:Candle[]){
  let net=0,total=0; const series:number[]=[];
  for(const c of candles){
    const buy=c.takerBuyQuote ?? c.quoteVolume*0.5;
    const delta=2*buy-c.quoteVolume; net+=delta; total+=c.quoteVolume; series.push(net);
  }
  let divergence:null|"bear"|"bull"=null;
  if(candles.length>=12){
    const mid=Math.floor(candles.length/2);
    if(candles.at(-1)!.close>candles[mid].close*1.002&&series.at(-1)!<series[mid]) divergence="bear";
    if(candles.at(-1)!.close<candles[mid].close*0.998&&series.at(-1)!>series[mid]) divergence="bull";
  }
  return {series,cvdPct:total>0?clamp(net/total):0,cvdNet:net,divergence};
}

export function computeTradeCvd(trades:FlowTrade[]) {
  let net=0,total=0,buy=0,sell=0;
  const sorted=[...trades].sort((a,b)=>a.time-b.time);
  const series:number[]=[];
  for(const t of sorted){
    const delta=t.isBuyerMaker?-t.notional:t.notional;
    net+=delta; total+=t.notional;
    if(delta>0) buy+=delta; else sell+=-delta;
    series.push(net);
  }
  return {net,total,pct:total>0?clamp(net/total):0,series,buy,sell,imbalance:total>0?(buy-sell)/total:0};
}

export function detectTradeDivergence(trades:FlowTrade[],priceNow:number,pricePrev:number){
  if(trades.length<20||pricePrev<=0)return null as "bear"|"bull"|null;
  const half=Math.floor(trades.length/2);
  const a=computeTradeCvd(trades.slice(0,half));
  const b=computeTradeCvd(trades.slice(half));
  if(priceNow>pricePrev*1.0015&&b.net<a.net)return "bear";
  if(priceNow<pricePrev*0.9985&&b.net>a.net)return "bull";
  return null;
}

export function analyzeBook(b:OrderBook,history:BookSnapshotLite[],previousLevels?:{bids:Map<number,number>;asks:Map<number,number>}):MicrostructureSnapshot{
  const bid=b.bids.reduce((s,x)=>s+x.notional,0),ask=b.asks.reduce((s,x)=>s+x.notional,0);
  const imb=ask>0?bid/ask:1,mid=b.mid;
  const spread=(((b.asks[0]?.price??mid)-(b.bids[0]?.price??mid))/Math.max(mid,1))*1e4;
  const snapshot={time:Date.now(),bidNotional:bid,askNotional:ask,imbalance:imb,mid,spreadBps:spread};
  const r=history.slice(-20),avg=r.length?r.reduce((s,x)=>s+x.imbalance,0)/r.length:1;
  const same=r.length?r.filter(x=>Math.sign(x.imbalance-1)===Math.sign(imb-1)).length/r.length:.5;
  let added=0,removed=0;
  const curB=new Map(b.bids.map(x=>[x.price,x.notional])),curA=new Map(b.asks.map(x=>[x.price,x.notional]));
  if(previousLevels){
    for(const [p,n] of curB){const old=previousLevels.bids.get(p)??0;if(n>old)added+=n-old;else removed+=old-n;}
    for(const [p,n] of curA){const old=previousLevels.asks.get(p)??0;if(n>old)added+=n-old;else removed+=old-n;}
    for(const [p,n] of previousLevels.bids){if(!curB.has(p))removed+=n;}
    for(const [p,n] of previousLevels.asks){if(!curA.has(p))removed+=n;}
  }
  const arTotal=added+removed;
  const addRemoveImbalance=arTotal>0?(added-removed)/arTotal:0;
  const spoofRisk=clamp((removed/Math.max(added+1,1)-1.5)/3);
  return {snapshot,bookDelta:clamp((imb-avg)*1.8),bookPersistence:clamp((same-.5)*2),liquidityAdded:added,liquidityRemoved:removed,addRemoveImbalance,absorptionScore:0,spoofRisk,sweep:0,volumeProfile:[],poc:0,vwap:0};
}

export interface AbsorptionAnalysis {
  score:number;
  side:'buy'|'sell'|null;
  confidence:number;
  aggressiveNotional:number;
  passiveNotional:number;
  flowImbalance:number;
  priceEfficiency:number;
  depthPersistence:number;
  replenishment:number;
}

/**
 * Detects absorption from three independent pieces of evidence:
 * 1) aggressive executed flow,
 * 2) poor price efficiency despite that flow, and
 * 3) persistence/replenishment of the resting book near the reaction area.
 * Positive score = buy-side absorption (sell pressure absorbed by bids).
 * Negative score = sell-side absorption (buy pressure absorbed by asks).
 */
export function detectAdvancedAbsorption(
  trades:FlowTrade[],
  candles:Candle[],
  l2History:L2Snapshot[],
  now=Date.now()
):AbsorptionAnalysis{
  const empty:AbsorptionAnalysis={score:0,side:null,confidence:0,aggressiveNotional:0,passiveNotional:0,flowImbalance:0,priceEfficiency:0,depthPersistence:0,replenishment:0};
  if(trades.length<40||candles.length<4||l2History.length<4)return empty;
  const recent=trades.filter(t=>now-t.time<=90_000).sort((a,b)=>a.time-b.time);
  if(recent.length<25)return empty;

  let buyAgg=0,sellAgg=0;
  for(const t of recent){
    if(t.isBuyerMaker) sellAgg+=t.notional; // seller is aggressor
    else buyAgg+=t.notional; // buyer is aggressor
  }
  const totalAgg=buyAgg+sellAgg;
  if(totalAgg<=0)return empty;
  const flowImbalance=(buyAgg-sellAgg)/totalAgg;

  const last=candles[candles.length-1],prev=candles[candles.length-2];
  const range=Math.max(last.high-last.low,Math.abs(last.close-prev.close),last.close*0.0001);
  const move=last.close-prev.close;
  const maxMove=recent.reduce((m,t)=>Math.max(m,Math.abs(t.price-(recent[0]?.price??t.price))),0);
  const priceEfficiency=Math.max(0,1-Math.min(1,Math.abs(move)/range));

  const window=l2History.slice(-8);
  const mid=last.close;
  const band=Math.max(mid*0.00035,range*0.35);
  let bidSeries:number[]=[]; let askSeries:number[]=[];
  for(const s of window){
    bidSeries.push(s.bids.filter(x=>Math.abs(x.price-mid)<=band).reduce((a,x)=>a+x.notional,0));
    askSeries.push(s.asks.filter(x=>Math.abs(x.price-mid)<=band).reduce((a,x)=>a+x.notional,0));
  }
  const avg=(xs:number[])=>xs.length?xs.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)/xs.length:0;
  const endBid=bidSeries.at(-1)??0,endAsk=askSeries.at(-1)??0;
  const bidPersist=avg(bidSeries)>0?Math.min(1,endBid/avg(bidSeries)):0;
  const askPersist=avg(askSeries)>0?Math.min(1,endAsk/avg(askSeries)):0;
  const bidRepl=bidSeries.length>1?clamp((endBid-(bidSeries[0]??0))/Math.max(avg(bidSeries),1)):0;
  const askRepl=askSeries.length>1?clamp((endAsk-(askSeries[0]??0))/Math.max(avg(askSeries),1)):0;
  const depthPersistence=(buyAgg>sellAgg?askPersist:bidPersist);
  const replenishment=(buyAgg>sellAgg?askRepl:bidRepl);

  const side: 'buy'|'sell'|null = sellAgg>buyAgg*1.35 ? 'buy' : buyAgg>sellAgg*1.35 ? 'sell' : null;
  if(!side||priceEfficiency<0.35||depthPersistence<0.55)return empty;

  const dominant=sellAgg>buyAgg?sellAgg:buyAgg;
  const dominance=Math.min(1,(dominant/Math.max(totalAgg,1)-0.5)*2);
  const raw=0.42*dominance+0.30*priceEfficiency+0.18*depthPersistence+0.10*Math.max(0,replenishment);
  const score=clamp(side==='buy'?raw:-raw);
  const confidence=clamp(raw*1.15);
  return {score,side,confidence,aggressiveNotional:dominant,passiveNotional:side==='buy'?endBid:endAsk,flowImbalance,priceEfficiency,depthPersistence,replenishment};
}

export function detectAbsorption(trades:FlowTrade[],candles:Candle[],l2History?:L2Snapshot[]):number{
  if(l2History&&l2History.length>=4) return detectAdvancedAbsorption(trades,candles,l2History).score;
  if(trades.length<30||candles.length<3)return 0;
  const recent=trades.filter(t=>Date.now()-t.time<=90_000);
  if(recent.length<20)return 0;
  const cvd=computeTradeCvd(recent),last=candles[candles.length-1],prev=candles[candles.length-2];
  const range=Math.max(last.high-last.low,Math.abs(last.close-prev.close));
  const move=last.close-prev.close,flow=Math.abs(cvd.imbalance);
  if(flow<0.18||range<=0)return 0;
  const ineff=1-Math.min(1,Math.abs(move)/range),score=ineff*flow*1.6;
  if(cvd.imbalance>0&&move<=0)return clamp(score);
  if(cvd.imbalance<0&&move>=0)return clamp(-score);
  return 0;
}

export function detectRealSweep(c:Candle[],lookback=8){
  if(c.length<lookback+3)return 0;
  const prior=c.slice(-(lookback+1),-1),hi=Math.max(...prior.map(x=>x.high)),lo=Math.min(...prior.map(x=>x.low)),x=c[c.length-1];
  if(x.high>hi&&x.close<hi)return -1;
  if(x.low<lo&&x.close>lo)return 1;
  return 0;
}

export function liquidationBurst(e:{time:number;notional:number}[],now=Date.now()){
  const recent=e.filter(x=>now-x.time<=300_000).reduce((s,x)=>s+x.notional,0);
  const prior=e.filter(x=>now-x.time>300_000&&now-x.time<=1_800_000).reduce((s,x)=>s+x.notional,0);
  const baseline=prior/5,ratio=baseline>0?recent/baseline:recent>0?3:0;
  return {recent,baseline,ratio,burst:clamp((Math.log10(Math.max(recent,1e3))-5)/2),acceleration:clamp((ratio-1)/4)};
}

export function buildVolumeProfile(candles:Candle[],bins=64){
  if(!candles.length)return {profile:[] as {price:number;volume:number}[],poc:0,vwap:0};
  const lo=Math.min(...candles.map(c=>c.low)),hi=Math.max(...candles.map(c=>c.high)),step=Math.max((hi-lo)/bins,1e-9);
  const profile=Array.from({length:bins},(_,i)=>({price:lo+(i+.5)*step,volume:0})); let pv=0,v=0;
  for(const c of candles){const idx=Math.max(0,Math.min(bins-1,Math.floor((c.close-lo)/step)));const q=Math.max(0,c.quoteVolume);profile[idx].volume+=q;pv+=c.close*q;v+=q;}
  const poc=profile.reduce((m,x)=>x.volume>m.volume?x:m,profile[0])?.price??0;
  return {profile,poc,vwap:v>0?pv/v:0};
}

export function clusterConvergence(
  ours:Array<{price:number;side:"long"|"short";estNotional:number}>,
  external:Array<{price:number;side:"long"|"short";estNotional:number}>|undefined,
  spot:number
){
  if(!external?.length||!ours.length)return {score:0,matches:[] as {side:"long"|"short";ours:number;external:number;distancePct:number}[]};
  const matches:{side:"long"|"short";ours:number;external:number;distancePct:number}[]=[];
  for(const o of ours){
    const same=external.filter(e=>e.side===o.side).sort((a,b)=>Math.abs(a.price-o.price)-Math.abs(b.price-o.price))[0];
    if(!same)continue;
    const d=Math.abs(same.price-o.price)/Math.max(spot,1)*100;
    if(d<=0.35)matches.push({side:o.side,ours:o.price,external:same.price,distancePct:d});
  }
  return {score:clamp(matches.length/4),matches};
}


============================================================================
FILE: src/lib/options.ts
============================================================================

/* ============================================================
   Binance Options: agregación real y defensiva.
   - Usa el catálogo activo de Binance Options.
   - Consulta OI/mark por contrato sin inventar strikes.
   - Amplía cobertura a varios vencimientos y strikes alrededor del ATM.
   - Guarda snapshots locales para calcular ΔOI observado.
   - Max Pain se calcula sobre el universo consultado; se marca parcial si
     la protección de rate-limit obliga a recortar el universo.
   ============================================================ */

export interface OptionsContractPoint {
  symbol: string;
  expiry: number;
  strike: number;
  type: "C" | "P";
  oiContracts: number;
  oiNotional: number;
  iv: number;
  delta?: number;
}

export interface OptionsSentiment {
  putCallRatio: number;
  putOi: number;
  callOi: number;
  totalOi: number;
  oiChangePct: number | null;
  indexPrice: number;
  contracts: number;
  expiries: number;
  nearestExpiry: number;
  atmIv: number;
  skew25d: number;
  maxPain: number;
  maxPainExpiry: number;
  quality: "live" | "partial";
}

const EAPI = "https://eapi.binance.com";
const CONTRACT_BTC = 0.01;
const MAX_EXPIRIES = 8;
const MAX_CONTRACTS = 240;
const STRIKE_BAND = 0.30;
const HISTORY_KEY = "liqradar.options.oi.history.v1";
const HISTORY_MAX = 240;

type Json = Record<string, unknown> | unknown[];

async function getJson<T>(url: string, timeoutMs = 7000): Promise<T> {
  const ctrl = new AbortController();
  const t = setTimeout(() => ctrl.abort(), timeoutMs);
  try {
    const res = await fetch(url, { signal: ctrl.signal });
    if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${res.status}`);
    return (await res.json()) as T;
  } finally {
    clearTimeout(t);
  }
}

interface OptSymbol {
  symbol: string;
  underlying: string;
  expiry: number;
  strikePrice: string;
  side: "CALL" | "PUT";
  status?: string;
}

interface ExchangeInfo { symbols?: OptSymbol[]; }
interface IndexResponse { indexPrice?: string; }
interface OiResponse { symbol: string; openInterest: number | string; time?: number; }
interface MarkResponse {
  symbol?: string;
  markPrice?: string;
  bidIV?: string;
  askIV?: string;
  delta?: string;
}

function safeLocalHistory(): { ts: number; totalOi: number }[] {
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(HISTORY_KEY);
    if (!raw) return [];
    const v = JSON.parse(raw);
    return Array.isArray(v) ? v.filter((x) => Number.isFinite(x?.ts) && Number.isFinite(x?.totalOi)) : [];
  } catch { return []; }
}

function saveLocalHistory(totalOi: number) {
  try {
    const next = [...safeLocalHistory(), { ts: Date.now(), totalOi }]
      .slice(-HISTORY_MAX);
    localStorage.setItem(HISTORY_KEY, JSON.stringify(next));
  } catch { /* storage can be disabled */ }
}

function observedOiChangePct(current: number): number | null {
  const history = safeLocalHistory();
  const previous = [...history].reverse().find((x) => Date.now() - x.ts >= 5 * 60_000);
  if (!previous || previous.totalOi <= 0) return null;
  return ((current - previous.totalOi) / previous.totalOi) * 100;
}

function median(values: number[]): number {
  const a = values.filter(Number.isFinite).sort((x, y) => x - y);
  if (!a.length) return 0;
  return a[Math.floor(a.length / 2)];
}

function computeMaxPain(points: OptionsContractPoint[], expiry: number): number {
  const subset = points.filter((p) => p.expiry === expiry && p.oiContracts > 0);
  const strikes = [...new Set(subset.map((p) => p.strike))].sort((a, b) => a - b);
  if (!strikes.length) return 0;
  let bestStrike = strikes[0];
  let bestPain = Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
  for (const test of strikes) {
    let pain = 0;
    for (const p of subset) {
      if (p.type === "C") pain += Math.max(test - p.strike, 0) * p.oiContracts * CONTRACT_BTC;
      else pain += Math.max(p.strike - test, 0) * p.oiContracts * CONTRACT_BTC;
    }
    if (pain < bestPain) { bestPain = pain; bestStrike = test; }
  }
  return bestStrike;
}

export async function fetchOptionsSentiment(): Promise<OptionsSentiment | null> {
  let indexPrice = 0;
  try {
    const idx = await getJson<IndexResponse[]>(`${EAPI}/eapi/v1/index?underlying=BTCUSDT`);
    indexPrice = Number(idx?.[0]?.indexPrice ?? 0);
  } catch { /* seguimos */ }

  let info: ExchangeInfo;
  try {
    info = await getJson<ExchangeInfo>(`${EAPI}/eapi/v1/exchangeInfo`);
  } catch {
    return null;
  }

  const active = (info.symbols ?? []).filter((s) =>
    s.underlying === "BTCUSDT" &&
    (s.status === undefined || s.status === "TRADING") &&
    Number.isFinite(Number(s.expiry)) && Number(s.expiry) > Date.now()
  );
  if (!active.length) return null;

  const expiries = [...new Set(active.map((s) => s.expiry))].sort((a, b) => a - b).slice(0, MAX_EXPIRIES);
  const inExpiries = active.filter((s) => expiries.includes(s.expiry));
  const atm = indexPrice > 0 ? indexPrice : median(inExpiries.map((s) => Number(s.strikePrice)));
  if (!(atm > 0)) return null;

  // Primero cubrimos strikes alrededor del ATM para cada vencimiento.
  const picked: OptSymbol[] = [];
  for (const expiry of expiries) {
    const same = inExpiries.filter((s) => s.expiry === expiry && Number.isFinite(Number(s.strikePrice)));
    const byDistance = [...same].sort((a, b) =>
      Math.abs(Number(a.strikePrice) - atm) - Math.abs(Number(b.strikePrice) - atm)
    );
    const bounded = byDistance.filter((s) => Math.abs(Number(s.strikePrice) - atm) / atm <= STRIKE_BAND);
    // Priorizamos ambos lados y distribuimos el presupuesto entre expiries.
    const quota = Math.ceil(MAX_CONTRACTS / expiries.length);
    picked.push(...bounded.slice(0, quota));
  }

  // Cobertura adicional: completar strikes cercanos si no llegamos al máximo.
  if (picked.length < Math.min(MAX_CONTRACTS, inExpiries.length)) {
    const used = new Set(picked.map((s) => s.symbol));
    for (const s of [...inExpiries].sort((a, b) => Math.abs(Number(a.strikePrice) - atm) - Math.abs(Number(b.strikePrice) - atm))) {
      if (picked.length >= MAX_CONTRACTS) break;
      if (!used.has(s.symbol) && Math.abs(Number(s.strikePrice) - atm) / atm <= STRIKE_BAND) {
        picked.push(s); used.add(s.symbol);
      }
    }
  }

  const fetchContract = async (s: OptSymbol): Promise<OptionsContractPoint | null> => {
    try {
      const [oi, mark] = await Promise.all([
        getJson<OiResponse>(`${EAPI}/eapi/v1/openInterest?symbol=${encodeURIComponent(s.symbol)}`),
        getJson<MarkResponse[]>(`${EAPI}/eapi/v1/mark?symbol=${encodeURIComponent(s.symbol)}`),
      ]);
      const oiContracts = Number(oi.openInterest);
      const iv = Number(mark?.[0]?.bidIV ?? mark?.[0]?.askIV ?? 0);
      const delta = Number(mark?.[0]?.delta ?? NaN);
      const strike = Number(s.strikePrice);
      if (!Number.isFinite(oiContracts) || !Number.isFinite(strike)) return null;
      return {
        symbol: s.symbol,
        expiry: s.expiry,
        strike,
        type: s.side === "CALL" ? "C" : "P",
        oiContracts,
        oiNotional: oiContracts * CONTRACT_BTC * strike,
        iv: Number.isFinite(iv) ? iv : 0,
        delta: Number.isFinite(delta) ? delta : undefined,
      };
    } catch { return null; }
  };

  const points: OptionsContractPoint[] = [];
  // Concurrencia moderada para no golpear rate limits innecesariamente.
  for (let i = 0; i < picked.length; i += 12) {
    const batch = await Promise.all(picked.slice(i, i + 12).map(fetchContract));
    points.push(...batch.filter((p): p is OptionsContractPoint => !!p));
  }
  if (!points.length) return null;

  const putOi = points.filter((p) => p.type === "P").reduce((a, p) => a + p.oiNotional, 0);
  const callOi = points.filter((p) => p.type === "C").reduce((a, p) => a + p.oiNotional, 0);
  const totalOi = putOi + callOi;
  const putContracts = points.filter((p) => p.type === "P").reduce((a, p) => a + p.oiContracts, 0);
  const callContracts = points.filter((p) => p.type === "C").reduce((a, p) => a + p.oiContracts, 0);
  if (totalOi <= 0) return null;

  const near = points.filter((p) => p.expiry === expiries[0]);
  const atmCandidates = points
    .filter((p) => Math.abs(p.strike - atm) / atm <= 0.015 && p.iv > 0)
    .sort((a, b) => Math.abs(a.strike - atm) - Math.abs(b.strike - atm));
  const atmIv = atmCandidates.length ? median(atmCandidates.slice(0, 4).map((p) => p.iv)) : 0;

  // 25-delta skew, si delta está disponible; fallback geométrico si no.
  const call25 = points.filter((p) => p.type === "C" && p.iv > 0 && p.delta != null)
    .sort((a, b) => Math.abs(Math.abs(b.delta ?? 0) - 0.25) - Math.abs(Math.abs(a.delta ?? 0) - 0.25))[0];
  const put25 = points.filter((p) => p.type === "P" && p.iv > 0 && p.delta != null)
    .sort((a, b) => Math.abs(Math.abs(a.delta ?? 0) - 0.25) - Math.abs(Math.abs(b.delta ?? 0) - 0.25))[0];
  const geometricCall25 = points.filter((p) => p.type === "C" && p.iv > 0)
    .sort((a, b) => Math.abs(a.strike / atm - 1.08) - Math.abs(b.strike / atm - 1.08))[0];
  const geometricPut25 = points.filter((p) => p.type === "P" && p.iv > 0)
    .sort((a, b) => Math.abs(a.strike / atm - 0.92) - Math.abs(b.strike / atm - 0.92))[0];
  const c25 = call25 ?? geometricCall25;
  const p25 = put25 ?? geometricPut25;
  const skew25d = c25 && p25 ? p25.iv - c25.iv : 0;

  const maxPainExpiry = expiries[0];
  const maxPain = computeMaxPain(near, maxPainExpiry) || atm;
  const oiChangePct = observedOiChangePct(totalOi);
  saveLocalHistory(totalOi);

  return {
    putCallRatio: callContracts > 0 ? putContracts / callContracts : 1,
    putOi,
    callOi,
    totalOi,
    oiChangePct,
    indexPrice: indexPrice || atm,
    contracts: points.length,
    expiries: new Set(points.map((p) => p.expiry)).size,
    nearestExpiry: maxPainExpiry,
    atmIv,
    skew25d,
    maxPain,
    maxPainExpiry,
    quality: points.length >= Math.min(24, MAX_CONTRACTS) && atmIv > 0 ? "live" : "partial",
  };
}


============================================================================
FILE: src/lib/paperAgent.ts
============================================================================

/* ============================================================
   Agente LiqRadar — motor autónomo de paper trading.
   Vigila el radar en vivo y actúa cuando se cumplen las reglas:
   abre posiciones paper, gestiona objetivo/invalidación y
   registra cada operación con PnL y múltiplos de R.
   ============================================================ */

import type { Verdict } from "./engine";

export type Autonomy = "alerts" | "auto";

export interface AgentRules {
  on: boolean;
  autonomy: Autonomy; // alerts = solo avisa · auto = abre posiciones paper
  minBias: number; // |sesgo| mínimo (0..100)
  minConf: number; // confianza mínima del radar
  minConfluence: number; // acuerdo MTF mínimo (0..3)
  riskPct: number; // % del capital paper arriesgado por operación
  cooldownSec: number; // espera mínima entre operaciones
  capital: number; // capital paper en USDT
}

export interface AgentPosition {
  id: string;
  side: "long" | "short";
  entry: number;
  qtyBtc: number;
  notional: number;
  target: number;
  stop: number;
  openedAt: number;
  bias: number;
  confidence: number;
  confluence: number;
  reason: string;
}

export type ExitReason = "objetivo" | "invalidación" | "giro" | "manual";

export interface AgentTrade {
  id: string;
  side: "long" | "short";
  entry: number;
  exit: number;
  pnlPct: number; // % de precio
  pnlR: number; // múltiplos de R
  pnlUsd: number;
  openedAt: number;
  closedAt: number;
  exitReason: ExitReason;
}

export interface LogEntry {
  id: string;
  t: number;
  kind: "info" | "open" | "close" | "warn" | "signal";
  text: string;
}

export interface AgentState {
  position: AgentPosition | null;
  trades: AgentTrade[];
  log: LogEntry[];
  equity: number; // capital paper actual
  curve: number[]; // curva de equity
  lastOpAt: number; // cooldown
}

export const DEFAULT_RULES: AgentRules = {
  on: false,
  autonomy: "alerts",
  minBias: 40,
  minConf: 55,
  minConfluence: 2,
  riskPct: 1,
  cooldownSec: 300,
  capital: 1000,
};

const LS_RULES = "liqradar-agent-rules-v1";
const LS_STATE = "liqradar-agent-state-v1";

export function loadAgentRules(): AgentRules {
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(LS_RULES);
    if (raw) return { ...DEFAULT_RULES, ...(JSON.parse(raw) as Partial<AgentRules>) };
  } catch {
    /* noop */
  }
  return { ...DEFAULT_RULES };
}

export function saveAgentRules(r: AgentRules) {
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(LS_RULES, JSON.stringify(r));
  } catch {
    /* noop */
  }
}

export function loadAgentState(capital: number): AgentState {
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(LS_STATE);
    if (raw) {
      const s = JSON.parse(raw) as AgentState;
      return { ...s, position: s.position ?? null, trades: s.trades ?? [], log: s.log ?? [], curve: s.curve ?? [capital] };
    }
  } catch {
    /* noop */
  }
  return { position: null, trades: [], log: [], equity: capital, curve: [capital], lastOpAt: 0 };
}

export function saveAgentState(s: AgentState) {
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(LS_STATE, JSON.stringify({ ...s, log: s.log.slice(0, 40), curve: s.curve.slice(-200) }));
  } catch {
    /* noop */
  }
}

const uid = () => `${Date.now()}-${Math.round(Math.random() * 1e6)}`;

export function pushLog(s: AgentState, kind: LogEntry["kind"], text: string): AgentState {
  return { ...s, log: [{ id: uid(), t: Date.now(), kind, text }, ...s.log].slice(0, 40) };
}

/* ¿La señal actual califica según las reglas? Devuelve razones legibles */
export function evaluateSignal(
  rules: AgentRules,
  v: Verdict,
  confluenceAgree: number,
  confluenceDir: "up" | "down" | "mixed" | null
): { go: boolean; reasons: string[] } {
  const reasons: string[] = [];
  let go = true;
  if (v.direction === "neutral") {
    go = false;
    reasons.push("el radar está en NEUTRO — sin rumbo definido");
  }
  if (Math.abs(v.scorePct) < rules.minBias) {
    go = false;
    reasons.push(`sesgo |${v.scorePct}| < mínimo ${rules.minBias}`);
  }
  if (v.confidence < rules.minConf) {
    go = false;
    reasons.push(`confianza ${v.confidence}% < mínima ${rules.minConf}%`);
  }
  if (confluenceAgree < rules.minConfluence) {
    go = false;
    reasons.push(`confluencia ${confluenceAgree}/3 < mínima ${rules.minConfluence}/3`);
  }
  if (confluenceDir && confluenceDir !== "mixed" && v.direction !== "neutral" && confluenceDir !== v.direction) {
    go = false;
    reasons.push(`los timeframes apuntan en contra (${confluenceDir === "up" ? "LONG" : "SHORT"})`);
  }
  if (!v.target || !v.invalidation) {
    go = false;
    reasons.push("el radar no tiene objetivo/invalidación definidos");
  }
  return { go, reasons };
}

export function openPosition(
  s: AgentState,
  rules: AgentRules,
  v: Verdict,
  confluenceAgree: number,
  spot: number
): AgentState {
  if (!v.target || !v.invalidation || v.direction === "neutral") return s;
  const side: "long" | "short" = v.direction === "up" ? "long" : "short";
  const riskDist = Math.abs(spot - v.invalidation.price);
  if (riskDist <= 0) return pushLog(s, "warn", "distancia a la invalidación nula — no se abrió posición");
  const riskUsd = (s.equity * rules.riskPct) / 100;
  const qtyBtc = riskUsd / riskDist;
  const notional = qtyBtc * spot;
  const pos: AgentPosition = {
    id: uid(),
    side,
    entry: spot,
    qtyBtc,
    notional,
    target: v.target.price,
    stop: v.invalidation.price,
    openedAt: Date.now(),
    bias: v.scorePct,
    confidence: v.confidence,
    confluence: confluenceAgree,
    reason: v.headline,
  };
  let next: AgentState = { ...s, position: pos, lastOpAt: Date.now() };
  next = pushLog(
    next,
    "open",
    `${side === "long" ? "▲ LONG" : "▼ SHORT"} abierto @ $${Math.round(spot).toLocaleString("en-US")} · ` +
      `${qtyBtc?.toFixed?.(5)} BTC · objetivo $${Math.round(pos.target).toLocaleString("en-US")} · stop $${Math.round(pos.stop).toLocaleString("en-US")} · ` +
      `riesgo $${riskUsd?.toFixed?.(0)} (${rules.riskPct}%)`
  );
  return next;
}

/* PnL en vivo de la posición abierta */
export function markToMarket(p: AgentPosition, spot: number): { pnlPct: number; pnlUsd: number; pnlR: number } {
  const dirMul = p.side === "long" ? 1 : -1;
  const pnlPct = ((spot - p.entry) / p.entry) * 100 * dirMul;
  const pnlUsd = (spot - p.entry) * p.qtyBtc * dirMul;
  const riskDistPct = (Math.abs(p.entry - p.stop) / p.entry) * 100;
  const pnlR = riskDistPct > 0 ? pnlPct / riskDistPct : 0;
  return { pnlPct, pnlUsd, pnlR };
}

/* Salidas: objetivo, invalidación o giro fuerte del radar en contra */
export function checkExits(
  s: AgentState,
  rules: AgentRules,
  v: Verdict,
  spot: number
): { exit: ExitReason | null; next: AgentState } {
  const p = s.position;
  if (!p) return { exit: null, next: s };

  const hitTarget = p.side === "long" ? spot >= p.target : spot <= p.target;
  const hitStop = p.side === "long" ? spot <= p.stop : spot >= p.stop;
  const against =
    v.direction !== "neutral" &&
    ((p.side === "long" && v.direction === "down") || (p.side === "short" && v.direction === "up")) &&
    Math.abs(v.scorePct) >= 30;

  let reason: ExitReason | null = null;
  if (hitTarget && hitStop) reason = Math.abs(spot - p.target) <= Math.abs(spot - p.stop) ? "objetivo" : "invalidación";
  else if (hitTarget) reason = "objetivo";
  else if (hitStop) reason = "invalidación";
  else if (against) reason = "giro";
  if (!reason) return { exit: null, next: s };

  return { exit: reason, next: closePosition(s, rules, spot, reason) };
}

export function closePosition(s: AgentState, rules: AgentRules, spot: number, reason: ExitReason): AgentState {
  const p = s.position;
  if (!p) return s;
  const { pnlPct, pnlUsd, pnlR } = markToMarket(p, spot);
  const trade: AgentTrade = {
    id: p.id,
    side: p.side,
    entry: p.entry,
    exit: spot,
    pnlPct,
    pnlR,
    pnlUsd,
    openedAt: p.openedAt,
    closedAt: Date.now(),
    exitReason: reason,
  };
  // el equity crece/decrece según el R obtenido sobre el % arriesgado
  const equity = Math.max(0, s.equity * (1 + (pnlR * rules.riskPct) / 100));
  let next: AgentState = {
    ...s,
    position: null,
    trades: [trade, ...s.trades].slice(0, 60),
    equity,
    curve: [...s.curve, equity].slice(-200),
    lastOpAt: Date.now(),
  };
  const win = pnlUsd >= 0;
  next = pushLog(
    next,
    "close",
    `${p.side === "long" ? "▲ LONG" : "▼ SHORT"} cerrado por ${reason.toUpperCase()} @ $${Math.round(spot).toLocaleString("en-US")} · ` +
      `${win ? "+" : ""}${pnlPct?.toFixed?.(2)}% · ${win ? "+" : ""}${pnlR?.toFixed?.(2)}R · ${win ? "+" : "−"}$${Math.abs(pnlUsd)?.toFixed?.(2)}`
  );
  return next;
}

/* Estadísticas agregadas del agente */
export function agentStats(s: AgentState) {
  const closed = s.trades;
  const wins = closed.filter((t) => t.pnlUsd >= 0).length;
  const losses = closed.length - wins;
  const winRate = closed.length > 0 ? (wins / closed.length) * 100 : null;
  const totalR = closed.reduce((a, t) => a + t.pnlR, 0);
  const totalUsd = closed.reduce((a, t) => a + t.pnlUsd, 0);
  const avgR = closed.length > 0 ? totalR / closed.length : 0;
  const best = closed.length > 0 ? Math.max(...closed.map((t) => t.pnlR)) : 0;
  const worst = closed.length > 0 ? Math.min(...closed.map((t) => t.pnlR)) : 0;
  const byObjective = closed.filter((t) => t.exitReason === "objetivo").length;
  const byStop = closed.filter((t) => t.exitReason === "invalidación").length;
  const byFlip = closed.filter((t) => t.exitReason === "giro").length;
  return { closed: closed.length, wins, losses, winRate, totalR, totalUsd, avgR, best, worst, byObjective, byStop, byFlip };
}


============================================================================
FILE: src/lib/positionFlow.ts
============================================================================

export type PositionRegime='LONG BUILD'|'SHORT BUILD'|'LONG UNWIND'|'SHORT UNWIND'|'NEUTRAL';
export interface PositionFlow { ratio:number; previous:number|null; deltaPct:number|null; z:number; regime:PositionRegime; positioningExtremity:number; confidence:number; }
const clamp=(x:number,a=-1,b=1)=>Math.max(a,Math.min(b,x));
export function computePositionFlow(current:number, history:number[], oiSlope5m:number, priceChange:number):PositionFlow {
  const clean=history.filter(Number.isFinite); const prev=clean.length?clean[clean.length-1]:null;
  const mean=clean.length?clean.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)/clean.length:current;
  const sd=clean.length>1?Math.sqrt(clean.reduce((s,v)=>s+(v-mean)**2,0)/Math.max(1,clean.length-1)):0;
  const deltaPct=prev&&prev!==0?((current-prev)/prev)*100:null;
  const z=sd>0?(current-mean)/sd:0;
  let regime:PositionRegime='NEUTRAL';
  if(oiSlope5m>0.25&&priceChange>0.15) regime='LONG BUILD';
  else if(oiSlope5m>0.25&&priceChange<-0.15) regime='SHORT BUILD';
  else if(oiSlope5m<-0.25&&priceChange>0.15) regime='SHORT UNWIND';
  else if(oiSlope5m<-0.25&&priceChange<-0.15) regime='LONG UNWIND';
  const positioningExtremity=clamp(Math.abs(z)/2,0,1);
  const confidence=clamp((positioningExtremity*0.5)+(Math.min(1,Math.abs(oiSlope5m)/1)*0.3)+(Math.min(1,Math.abs(priceChange)/1)*0.2),0,1);
  return {ratio:current,previous:prev,deltaPct,z,regime,positioningExtremity,confidence};
}

/* Alertas sonoras del radar (WebAudio, sin dependencias).
   El contexto se crea/resume con el primer gesto del usuario. */

let ctx: AudioContext | null = null;

function ensureCtx(): AudioContext | null {
  try {
    if (!ctx) {
      const AC = window.AudioContext ?? (window as unknown as { webkitAudioContext?: typeof AudioContext }).webkitAudioContext;
      if (!AC) return null;
      ctx = new AC();
    }
    if (ctx.state === "suspended") void ctx.resume();
    return ctx;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

// desbloquea el audio con el primer clic en cualquier parte
if (typeof window !== "undefined") {
  window.addEventListener("pointerdown", () => ensureCtx(), { once: true });
}

function tone(freq: number, at: number, dur: number, gain = 0.055, type: OscillatorType = "sine") {
  const c = ensureCtx();
  if (!c) return;
  const o = c.createOscillator();
  const g = c.createGain();
  o.type = type;
  o.frequency.setValueAtTime(freq, c.currentTime + at);
  g.gain.setValueAtTime(0, c.currentTime + at);
  g.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(gain, c.currentTime + at + 0.012);
  g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, c.currentTime + at + dur);
  o.connect(g).connect(c.destination);
  o.start(c.currentTime + at);
  o.stop(c.currentTime + at + dur + 0.05);
}

/** giro de rumbo: up = chirrido ascendente · down = descendente */
export function playFlip(dir: "up" | "down") {
  if (dir === "up") {
    tone(523, 0, 0.14);
    tone(784, 0.12, 0.2);
  } else {
    tone(523, 0, 0.14);
    tone(330, 0.12, 0.2);
  }
}

/** entrada en zona magnética: blip corto */
export function playMagnet() {
  tone(880, 0, 0.07, 0.04, "triangle");
  tone(1174, 0.09, 0.09, 0.03, "triangle");
}

/** señal francotirador: triple blip urgente */
export function playSniper() {
  tone(988, 0, 0.07, 0.05, "square");
  tone(988, 0.11, 0.07, 0.05, "square");
  tone(1319, 0.22, 0.16, 0.05, "square");
}

/** confirmación (al activar sonido / probar webhook) */
export function playConfirm() {
  tone(660, 0, 0.09);
}

const KEY = "liqradar-sound";

export function loadSoundPref(): boolean {
  try {
    return localStorage.getItem(KEY) === "1";
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}

export function saveSoundPref(on: boolean) {
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(KEY, on ? "1" : "0");
  } catch {
    /* noop */ }
}


============================================================================
FILE: src/lib/spoofDetector.ts
============================================================================

import type { FlowTrade } from './microstructure';

type Side = 'bid' | 'ask';
export interface LiquidityLifecycleEvent {
  time:number;
  side:Side;
  price:number;
  before:number;
  after:number;
  delta:number;
}
export interface SuspiciousPull {
  side:Side;
  price:number;
  notional:number;
  lifetimeMs:number;
  tradeTouched:boolean;
  sizeRatio:number;
  repeatedCount:number;
  score:number;
}
export interface SpoofAnalysis {
  score:number;
  suspiciousPulls:SuspiciousPull[];
  addedNotional:number;
  removedNotional:number;
  shortLivedRemovedNotional:number;
  untouchedRemovedNotional:number;
  repeatedLevelCount:number;
  status:'low'|'elevated'|'high';
}

const clamp=(x:number,a=0,b=1)=>Math.max(a,Math.min(b,x));

interface LiveLevel { side:Side; price:number; notional:number; addedAt:number; addedNotional:number; }

/**
 * Detects suspicious liquidity pulls, NOT definitive spoofing.
 * Evidence requires a large add, fast removal, no nearby trade-through, and/or repetition.
 */
export function analyzeLiquidityPulls(
  events:LiquidityLifecycleEvent[],
  trades:FlowTrade[],
  now=Date.now(),
  horizonMs=30_000
):SpoofAnalysis{
  const ev=events.filter(e=>now-e.time<=horizonMs && e.before>=0 && e.after>=0 && e.price>0);
  if(!ev.length) return {score:0,suspiciousPulls:[],addedNotional:0,removedNotional:0,shortLivedRemovedNotional:0,untouchedRemovedNotional:0,repeatedLevelCount:0,status:'low'};
  const adds=ev.filter(e=>e.after>e.before).map(e=>e.after-e.before);
  const sorted=[...adds].sort((a,b)=>a-b);
  const median=sorted.length?sorted[Math.floor(sorted.length/2)]:0;
  const states=new Map<string,LiveLevel>();
  const pulls:SuspiciousPull[]=[];
  let addedNotional=0,removedNotional=0,shortLived=0,untouched=0;
  const keyOf=(s:Side,p:number)=>`${s}:${Math.round(p*100)/100}`;
  for(const e of ev.sort((a,b)=>a.time-b.time)){
    const k=keyOf(e.side,e.price);
    const delta=e.after-e.before;
    if(delta>0){
      addedNotional+=delta;
      const prev=states.get(k);
      if(prev) prev.notional=Math.max(prev.notional, e.after);
      else states.set(k,{side:e.side,price:e.price,notional:e.after,addedAt:e.time,addedNotional:delta});
      continue;
    }
    if(delta<0){
      const removed=Math.min(Math.abs(delta),Math.max(0,e.before));
      removedNotional+=removed;
      const st=states.get(k);
      const lifetime=st?Math.max(0,e.time-st.addedAt):horizonMs;
      const base=st?.addedNotional ?? Math.max(removed,1);
      const sizeRatio=base/Math.max(median,1);
      const tradeTouched=trades.some(t=>t.time>= (st?.addedAt??e.time-lifetime) && t.time<=e.time && (e.side==='ask'?t.price>=e.price:t.price<=e.price));
      const fast=st!==undefined && lifetime<=3000;
      const large=sizeRatio>=3;
      const majority=removed>=Math.max(base*0.65,1);
      if(fast) shortLived+=removed;
      if(!tradeTouched) untouched+=removed;
      if(st && fast && large && majority && !tradeTouched){
        const repetition=ev.filter(x=>x.side===e.side && Math.abs(x.price-e.price)<=Math.max(e.price*0.00005,1) && x.time<e.time && x.after>x.before).length;
        const score=clamp(0.35*clamp(lifetime<=500?1:lifetime<=1500?0.8:0.55)+0.3*clamp(sizeRatio/8)+0.2*(tradeTouched?0:1)+0.15*clamp(repetition/3));
        pulls.push({side:e.side,price:e.price,notional:removed,lifetimeMs:lifetime,tradeTouched,sizeRatio,repeatedCount:repetition,score});
      }
      if(e.after===0) states.delete(k); else if(st) st.notional=e.after;
    }
  }
  const merged=new Map<string,SuspiciousPull>();
  for(const p of pulls){const k=keyOf(p.side,p.price);const old=merged.get(k);if(!old||p.score>old.score) merged.set(k,p);}
  const suspicious=[...merged.values()].sort((a,b)=>b.score-a.score).slice(0,20);
  const repeatedLevelCount=suspicious.filter(x=>x.repeatedCount>=2).length;
  const mass=removedNotional>0?shortLived/removedNotional:0;
  const untouchedRatio=removedNotional>0?untouched/removedNotional:0;
  const top=suspicious.slice(0,5).reduce((s,x)=>s+x.score,0)/Math.max(1,Math.min(5,suspicious.length));
  const score=clamp(0.45*top+0.3*clamp(mass)+0.25*clamp(untouchedRatio));
  const status=score>=0.7?'high':score>=0.45?'elevated':'low';
  return {score,suspiciousPulls:suspicious,addedNotional,removedNotional,shortLivedRemovedNotional:shortLived,untouchedRemovedNotional:untouched,repeatedLevelCount,status};
}


============================================================================
FILE: src/main.tsx

import React, { Component, ErrorInfo, useEffect } from "react";
import ReactDOM from "react-dom/client";
import "./index.css";
import App from "./App.tsx";

class BootErrorBoundary extends Component<{ children: React.ReactNode }, { error: Error | null }> {
  state = { error: null as Error | null };
  static getDerivedStateFromError(error: Error) { return { error }; }
  componentDidCatch(error: Error, info: ErrorInfo) {
    console.error("[LIQRADAR BOOT ERROR]", error, info);
    const pre = document.getElementById("boot-error-details");
    if (pre) pre.textContent = `${error.name}: ${error.message}\n\n${error.stack ?? ""}`;
  }
  render() {
    if (this.state.error) {
      return React.createElement("div", {
        style: { minHeight: "100vh", padding: "36px", background: "#050b16", color: "#e9f1ff", fontFamily: "system-ui, sans-serif" }
      },
        React.createElement("div", { style: { maxWidth: 900, margin: "0 auto", border: "1px solid rgba(255,77,109,.35)", borderRadius: 12, padding: 24, background: "#07101f" } },
          React.createElement("h1", { style: { color: "#ff7d95", marginTop: 0 } }, "LIQRADAR encontró un error al iniciar"),
          React.createElement("p", null, "El servidor está funcionando, pero un componente del frontend falló. El error exacto aparece abajo."),
          React.createElement("pre", { id: "boot-error-details", style: { whiteSpace: "pre-wrap", overflowX: "auto", fontSize: 12, lineHeight: 1.5, background: "#050b16", padding: 16, borderRadius: 8, color: "#ffb6c1" } }, `${this.state.error.name}: ${this.state.error.message}\n\n${this.state.error.stack ?? ""}`),
          React.createElement("button", { onClick: () => location.reload(), style: { marginTop: 12, padding: "10px 14px", borderRadius: 8, border: "1px solid #2fd6a5", background: "transparent", color: "#5ef2c4", cursor: "pointer" } }, "Recargar")
        )
      );
    }
    return this.props.children;
  }
}

function BootMarker() {
  useEffect(() => {
    const el = document.getElementById("boot-screen");
    if (el) el.remove();
    document.documentElement.dataset.liqradarBooted = "1";
  }, []);
  return <App />;
}

const root = ReactDOM.createRoot(document.getElementById("root")!);
root.render(<BootErrorBoundary><BootMarker /></BootErrorBoundary>);

import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
const root = path.resolve(new URL("..", import.meta.url).pathname);
const files=[];
function walk(d){for(const n of fs.readdirSync(d)){const p=path.join(d,n),s=fs.statSync(p);if(s.isDirectory()&&!['node_modules','dist','.git'].includes(n))walk(p);else if(/\.(ts|tsx|js|mjs)$/.test(p))files.push(p);}}
walk(path.join(root,"src"));
const forbidden=["simFunding","simKlines","simTick","simLiqEvent","simSeries"];
const hits=[];
for(const f of files){const t=fs.readFileSync(f,"utf8");for(const token of forbidden)if(t.includes(token))hits.push(`${path.relative(root,f)} contains ${token}`);}
console.log(`SOURCE_FILES=${files.length}`);
console.log(`FORBIDDEN_MARKET_SIMULATION_REMAINS=${hits.length}`);
if(hits.length){console.log(hits.join("\n"));process.exitCode=1;}

import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { execFileSync } from "node:child_process";

const root = path.resolve(new URL("..", import.meta.url).pathname);
const files=[];
function walk(d){for(const n of fs.readdirSync(d)){const p=path.join(d,n),s=fs.statSync(p);if(s.isDirectory()&&!['node_modules','dist','.git'].includes(n))walk(p);else if(/\.(ts|tsx)$/.test(p))files.push(p);}}
walk(path.join(root,"src"));

const forbidden=["simKlines","simTick","simLiqEvent","simSeries"];
const hits=[];
for(const f of files){
  const t=fs.readFileSync(f,"utf8");
  for(const token of forbidden){
    if(t.includes(token)) hits.push(`${path.relative(root,f)} contains ${token}`);
  }
}
console.log(`SOURCE_FILES=${files.length}`);
console.log(`FORBIDDEN_MARKET_SIMULATION_REMAINS=${hits.length}`);
if(hits.length) console.log(hits.join("\n"));

try { execFileSync("node",["--check",path.join(root,"server.mjs")],{stdio:"pipe"}); console.log("SERVER_SYNTAX=OK"); }
catch(e){ console.log("SERVER_SYNTAX=FAIL"); process.exitCode=1; }

if(hits.length) process.exitCode=1;
